Extensive experiments on six stocks and cryptocurrency assets demonstrate that F$^2$Agent consistently outperforms 16 competitive baselines across multiple trading metrics, with over 20% relative improvement in annualized return on average. Notably, F$^2$Agent delivers returns of 120.48% on GOOG and 148.41% on TSLA, demonstrating its efficacy and robustness in varying market dynamics.

</details>


### [104] [A Two-Tier Perspective on Inference-Time Parallelism in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05791)
*Zihan Xu,Haolin Tian,Hai Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了TIPEX框架，统一了多智能体系统推理时的两种并行策略（副本并行与结构并行），系统性地研究了它们对系统准确性、延迟和计算成本的影响，发现合理的并行策略组合能显著提升性能，但过度并行化未必有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的多智能体系统在推理时需要多次模型调用和复杂的协调机制，其执行策略直接影响系统的准确性、延迟和计算成本。虽然并行执行可以提升推理效率，但不同层级的并行机制（副本并行与结构并行）的角色及其相互关系缺乏系统性的统一组织与协调研究。

Method: 提出TIPEX可控执行框架，将多智能体系统的并行性建模为两个决策层：（1）副本并行：在任务层探索多条完整解路径；（2）结构并行：通过任务分解在单条解路径内实现并发执行。框架在统一的执行语义下协调这两种并行级别，支持对不同并行策略和参数配置进行系统性组合与分析。

Result: 在GAIA基准上的系统实验表明，推理时并行性能够以增加令牌消耗为代价显著提升准确性并降低端到端延迟。进一步分析发现：副本并行与结构并行在不同任务复杂度下呈互补效应，中等难度任务最能从二者的协调中获益，而过度激进的并行策略未必带来更优性能。

Conclusion: 推理时并行性是提升多智能体系统效能的有效手段，但需要根据任务复杂度进行合理配置与协调；TIPEX框架为系统性研究和优化并行策略提供了统一的分析工具与实验基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-driven multi-agent systems typically require multiple model invocations and complex coordination during inference, and their execution strategies directly affect system accuracy, latency, and computational cost. Parallel execution provides a means to improve inference-time efficiency. From the perspective of inference-time execution, this paper models parallelism in multi-agent systems as two distinct levels of decision processes: Replica Parallelism, which explores multiple complete solution paths at the task level, and Structural Parallelism, which enables concurrent execution within a single solution path through task decomposition. However, the roles of different forms of parallelism and their interrelationships still lack systematic study in terms of unified organization and coordination. We therefore propose TIPEX, a controllable execution framework that unifies these two levels of parallelism and coordinates their roles within the inference process under a unified execution semantics while supporting systematic combinations and analyses of different parallel strategies and parameter configurations. Systematic experiments on the GAIA benchmark demonstrate that inference-time parallelism can significantly improve accuracy and reduce end-to-end latency at the cost of increased token consumption. Further analysis shows that Replica and Structural Parallelism exhibit complementary effects across task complexities, with tasks of intermediate difficulty benefiting most from their coordination, while overly aggressive parallel strategies do not necessarily yield better performance.

</details>


### [105] [Certifying Collective Reasoning in Multi-Agent Systems via Koopman Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05956)
*Nuzhat Khan,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Koopman算子理论的框架，通过谱分析为LLM多智能体辩论投票系统提供可证明的收敛保证、决策归因和压缩审计，实现了对集体智能的可信认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体集合系统虽然通过辩论和投票提高了任务准确性，但在系统层面仍是黑箱：缺乏收敛性测试原则、无法确定所需轮数上限、无法忠实解释决策驱动因素。这些系统的智能行为存在于交互中而非单个智能体，亟需理论框架来保证其可信性。

Method: 将多智能体集体视为通信图上的非线性动力系统，采用Koopman算子理论构建其精确线性表示。从交互轨迹估计Koopman转移算子，通过其谱分析获得三个机器可验证证书：(1)次主导特征值λ₂确定推理内在时间尺度和可计算的收敛截止时间；(2)特征向量识别智能体的连贯派系；(3)|λ₂|验证解释有效性；(4)主导谱坐标形成压缩可审计的消息基。

Result: 在注意力-共识模型上验证：(1)截止时间与观测收敛呈log-log相关性0.93，96%的24种配置中成功界定收敛；(2)当谱证明亚稳态时归因完全准确；(3)32个坐标中的8个以99.7%保真度保持决策；(4)从15场辩论学习的证书在60/60场未见辩论上均有效；(5)CPU上分钟级运行，谱认证成为可信集体推理的实用层。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子的谱认证框架成功解决了LLM多智能体系统的黑箱问题，提供了收敛保证、决策归因和压缩审计的理论保证，在保持计算效率的同时实现了集体智能的可信化，为未来多智能体系统的安全部署提供了重要技术支撑。

Abstract: Orchestrated collectives of large language model (LLM) agents that debate and vote are an emerging form of computational intelligence: the intelligent behaviour resides in the \emph{interaction}, not in any single agent. They improve task accuracy, yet remain black boxes at the system level: there is no principled test of convergence, no bound on the rounds needed, and no faithful account of what drove a decision. This paper develops a novel framework based on Koopman operator theory and validates its theoretical guarantees on multi-agent consensus dynamics. Treating the collective as one nonlinear dynamical system on a communication graph, we read its essential behaviour off the spectrum of its Koopman transfer operator, an exact linear representation of the nonlinear dynamics estimated from interaction traces. The spectrum yields three machine-checkable certificates: the sub-dominant eigenvalue $λ_2$ fixes the intrinsic timescale of reasoning and yields a convergence deadline computable \emph{before} the debate runs; its eigenvector names the coherent factions the collective reasons in, and $|λ_2|$ certifies when that explanation is valid; and the leading spectral coordinates form a compressed, auditable message basis. On an attention-consensus model, the deadline tracks observed convergence with log--log correlation $0.93$ and bounds it in 96\% of 24 configurations; attribution is exact whenever the spectrum certifies metastability; eight of 32 coordinates preserve the decision at 99.7\% fidelity; and a certificate learned from 15 debates held on 60/60 held-out debates. The study runs in minutes on a CPU, making spectral certification a practical layer for trustworthy collective reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [ASGE-RR: Agentic Service Graph Embedding with Revisable Reservations for Dynamic AI-Agent Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06033)
*Trond Vatten,Yuming Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出ASGE-RR控制器，通过可修正的预留机制保护未来可能出现的调用所需资源，在WAN测试床上相比基线方法提高了10%的工作流完成价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体工作流在执行过程中通过远程调用访问分布式模型、存储和工具，形成智能体服务图（ASG）。与传统的服务请求不同，这些依赖调用仅在运行时才被揭示，导致当前可见调用的资源分配可能占用后续更高价值工作流所需的容量，这构成了一个新的在线网络控制问题。

Method: 本文将这一挑战形式化为智能体服务图嵌入（ASGE）问题，并提出ASGE-RR在线控制器。该控制器采用可修正的预留机制，基于预测的工作流延续评估候选副本和路径映射，在容量、成本和截止时间约束下保护可能未来调用所需的资源，并根据新执行信息动态更新预留。

Result: 使用OpenHands和GPT Researcher工作流（基于gpt-5.6-luna）在Docker和WAN测试床上进行评估。所有AI智能体任务至少暴露一个可在连接建立前被引导的运行时揭示依赖调用。在小规模实验环境中，ASGE-RR在WAN测试床上相比滚动时域控制器和当前调用引导控制器完成了最多10%更多的工作流价值。

Conclusion: 运行时揭示的工作流结构创造了新的网络控制机会：为可能发生的未来调用保护资源，能够使更多AI智能体工作流按时完成，验证了预留机制在动态工作流调度中的有效性。

Abstract: AI-agent workflows often involve remote calls to models, memory stores, and tools distributed across a network. As execution progresses, these dependency calls collectively form an agentic service graph (ASG). Unlike traditional service requests, many dependency calls are revealed only at runtime. Consequently, allocating resources to a currently visible call may consume capacity later needed by a call from a higher-value workflow. We formulate this challenge as Agentic Service Graph Embedding (ASGE), an online network-control problem that maps runtime-revealed workflow calls to service replicas and network paths under capacity, cost and deadline constraints. We present ASGE-RR, an online ASGE controller with revisable reservations. ASGE-RR protects capacity for likely future calls while enforcing the constraints. ASGE-RR evaluates candidate replica-and-path mappings against predicted workflow continuations and updates reservations as new execution information becomes available.
  We evaluate ASGE-RR using OpenHands and GPT Researcher workflows executed with gpt-5.6-luna and replayed over in two complementary experimental environments, a controlled Docker testbed and a WAN testbed. The investigation shows that all the evaluated AI-agent tasks expose at least one runtime-revealed dependency call that can be steered before connection establishment. Exploiting this control point, even though the experimental environments are small-scale, ASGE-RR already demonstrates noticeable potential: It completes (up to) 10% more workflow value than a same-information rolling-horizon controller and a current-call steering controller on the WAN testbed. The results suggest that runtime-revealed workflow structure creates a new network control opportunity: protecting resources for likely future calls allows more AI-agent workflows to finish in time.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [EdgeXpert: An Edge Device for Memory-Efficient LLM Inference with Mixture-of-Experts and Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05303)
*Sangwoo Ha,Hyunwoo Seo,Yurim Jo,Youngjin Moon,Hoi-Jun Yoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出EdgeXpert，一种软硬件协同设计的LLM加速器，解决了推测解码与混合专家模型结合时的不兼容问题，在28nm工艺下实现了56.3%延迟降低和44.1%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在端侧部署时，前馈网络层的外部内存访问(EMA)成为主要瓶颈。推测解码和混合专家模型(MoE)虽各有优势，但两者结合时存在不兼容性问题，限制了端侧LLM的部署效率。

Method: 提出EdgeXpert软硬件协同设计方案：1)预填充阶段：采用提示级专家重用策略，通过轻量级编码器识别重要token构建共享专家集，降低专家EMA；2)解码阶段：利用深度感知专家合并技术，仅加载显著通道并通过计算校准恢复精度，避免额外的内存访问。

Result: 在Samsung 28nm工艺、800MHz频率下实现，相比现有工作延迟降低最高56.3%，能耗降低44.1%，同时保持接近基线的准确率。

Conclusion: EdgeXpert成功解决了推测解码与MoE结合的不兼容问题，通过提示级专家重用和深度感知专家合并技术，显著降低了端侧LLM部署的延迟和能耗，为个性化边缘应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: On-device deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become essential for personalized edge applications. A primary bottleneck is external memory access (EMA) in feed-forward network (FFN) layers. Speculative decoding and mixture-of-experts (MoE) are promising solutions. Speculative decoding reduces the number of decoding stages by generating multiple tokens per stage, and MoE minimizes per-stage cost through sparse expert activation. However, there is an incompatibility when combining these two techniques. We propose EdgeXpert, a software-hardware co-designed LLM accelerator that resolves this incompatibility. In the prefill stage, the prompt-wise expert reuse reformulates routing as prompt-level expert reuse rather than independent per-token expert selection. It identifies important tokens using a lightweight encoder, constructs a shared expert set from them, and routes less important tokens with a reduced expert budget to lower expert EMA. In the decode stage, depth-aware expert coalescing exploits the contextual similarity and mutual exclusivity of same-depth candidate tokens. Rather than loading the union of all required channels, EdgeXpert loads only salient channels and applies computational calibration to recover accuracy without additional memory access. Synthesized in Samsung 28nm technology at 800 MHz, EdgeXpert achieves up to 56.3% latency reduction and 44.1% energy reduction compared to prior works, while maintaining near-baseline accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [PowerScope: ML-based Intra-Cycle Power Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05339)
*Jayanth Balasubramanian,Sujay Pandit,Radha Vaidya,Anand Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了PowerScope，首个基于机器学习的时钟周期内功耗估算框架，在纯RTL仿真基础上实现了亚时钟周期精度的功耗分析，速度提升约80倍且保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的商业门级功耗分析工具计算成本高昂，难以随设计规模和工作负载长度扩展；而现有机器学习功耗估算方法仅支持平均功耗或周级功耗估算，无法满足电源分配网络设计、动态电压压降分析和侧信道安全评估等任务对亚时钟周期时间分辨率功耗估算的需求。

Method: 提出PowerScope框架，这是首个基于机器学习的时钟周期内功耗估算方法。该框架在推理阶段完全基于RTL仿真轨迹工作，消除了针对每个工作负载进行后布局门级仿真和功耗分析的需求，实现了对亚时钟周期时间分辨率的功耗预测。

Result: 在多样化的基准测试集上，PowerScope相比商业后布局门级功耗估算实现了5.88%的中位数绝对百分比误差和9%的平均绝对百分比误差，同时运行速度提升约80倍。实验证明其预测结果可可靠用于芯片前功耗侧信道泄露评估任务。

Conclusion: PowerScope成功填补了亚时钟周期功耗估算的空白，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了计算效率，为电源分配网络设计、动态电压压降分析和侧信道安全评估等关键任务提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Power estimation at sub-clock-cycle temporal resolutions is critical for tasks such as power delivery network (PDN) design, dynamic voltage droop analysis, and pre-silicon power side-channel security evaluation. Designers commonly rely on commercial post-layout gate-level power analysis tools for these tasks, but these flows are computationally expensive and scale poorly with design size and workload length. Machine learning (ML)-based power estimation frameworks have shown promise in accelerating power estimation, but prior efforts only address average power or per-cycle power estimation. We propose PowerScope, the first ML-based intra-cycle power estimation framework. PowerScope operates purely on RTL simulation traces at inference time, eliminating the need for post-layout gate-level simulation and power analysis per workload. Across a diverse benchmark suite, PowerScope achieves 5.88% median and 9% mean absolute percentage error compared to commercial post-layout gate-level power estimates while running ~80x faster. We further demonstrate that PowerScope's predictions can be reliably used for the downstream task of pre-silicon power side-channel leakage assessment.

</details>


### [109] [PLoRA: An NDP-Enhanced Pooled-Memory System for Cost-Efficient Multi-LoRA Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05483)
*Zhongkai Yu,Ohm Rishabh Venkatachalam,Zheng Wang,Yikai Li,Yichen Lin,Zihao Yu,Yuke Wang,Liu Liu,Xulong Tang,Shuyi Pei,Yangwook Kang,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PLoRA，一种基于近数据计算（NDP）和池化内存的高效多LoRA服务系统，通过GPU驱动的读-计算接口和智能内存管理策略，在H100上服务1000个适配器时实现平均比S-LoRA低6.6倍的解码延迟，仅增加不到3.4%的设备面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在多LoRA服务中面临核心挑战：(1)工作负载特性与GPU硬件设计倒置——需要TB级内存但仅需数十TFLOPS算力；(2)现有系统通过PCIe从CPU DRAM分阶段加载适配器，每次访问都需要内核停止和主机端拷贝，且容量受限于主板DIMM插槽；(3)新型内存语义架构（CXL和NVLink）正在向池化内存发展，支持加速器直接进行加载/存储访问，结合近数据计算可在池化数据旁放置计算单元，但如何在此类硬件上服务多LoRA工作负载尚未被探索。

Method: PLoRA采用NDP增强的池化内存架构，核心设计包括：(1)将适配器和KV缓存保存在内存池中，通过GPU自身加载/存储驱动的读-计算接口，仅通过链路返回压缩后的结果；(2)GPU内存管理系统为每个适配器在四种LoRA和两种注意力执行策略间进行选择；(3)基于链路参数化的成本模型指导，将最关键的性能字节缓存在GPU内存中；(4)设计可从CXL级到NVLink级架构无缝运行，利用过剩带宽换取池化容量而非速度。

Result: 在单个H100 GPU服务1000个适配器的场景下，PLoRA在所有测试模型和工作负载上均实现最低解码延迟，平均比实际机器上的S-LoRA低6.6倍，且仅增加不到3.4%的设备面积。链路带宽不再是瓶颈：短上下文场景下吞吐量在32 GB/s即饱和（仅为CXL 3.1带宽的四分之一）。扩展性良好：随着张量并行中适配器流量的分片，单GPU需求从7B部署降至建模的1.2T部署。

Conclusion: PLoRA成功证明了NDP增强的池化内存架构能够高效解决多LoRA服务的核心挑战，通过智能的内存管理和执行策略选择显著降低了延迟并提升了成本效率。该设计具有优秀的硬件适应性，可在不同级别的内存语义架构上运行，表明未来多LoRA服务系统的设计应转向池化内存与近数据计算的协同架构方向。

Abstract: Multi-LoRA serving is how one base model becomes thousands of specialized variants, one adapter per user, task, or agent, and the deployments can hold 1000-plus adapters. Serving them is hard because the workload inverts what GPUs provide: terabytes of memory against only tens of TFLOPS, and because every published system stages its adapters from CPU DRAM over PCIe, where each access pays a kernel stop and a host-run copy and capacity ends at the motherboard's DIMM slots. Meanwhile, memory-semantic fabrics such as CXL and NVLink are converging on pooled memory that an accelerator addresses with its own loads and stores, and near-data processing (NDP) can place compute beside the pooled data. How to serve multi-LoRA workloads on such hardware remains unexplored.
  This paper introduces PLoRA, an NDP-enhanced pooled-memory system for cost-efficient multi-LoRA serving. PLoRA keeps adapters and KV cache in the pool and returns only reduced results over the link, through a read-compute interface the GPU drives with its own loads and stores. Above this architecture, a GPU memory management system picks among four LoRA and two attention execution strategies for each adapter and caches the most performance-critical bytes in GPU memory, guided by a link-parameterized cost model. On one H100 serving 1000 adapters, PLoRA attains the lowest decode latency on every model and workload we measure, averaging 6.6x below a real-machine S-LoRA at under 3.4% added device area. The link itself stops mattering: throughput saturates at 32 GB/s on short contexts, a quarter of CXL 3.1, and the verdict survives scale: per-GPU demand falls from 7B to a modeled 1.2T deployment once adapter traffic shards with the tensor parallelism. The design runs unchanged from CXL-class to NVLink-class fabrics, and surplus bandwidth buys pooled capacity rather than speed.

</details>


### [110] [Zero-Instruction Sensor Reads: Register-Mapped Peripherals and Hardware PWM on a Five-Stage Soft Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05638)
*Nathanael Ren*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 通过应用驱动的专用化设计，将传感器读取开销降至零指令，控制循环周期从91降至43周期，但实时性并非主要动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过专用化设计减少软件复杂度和指令计数，而非满足实时性要求；因为现有系统已能通过单周期I/O区域满足实时性约束。

Method: 在五级流水线软处理器上实施两项专用化：1）将两个高频外设输入直接映射为架构寄存器状态，每周期由硬件写入；2）将四路PWM通道卸载至硬件，从导出寄存器连续驱动。

Result: 传感器读取零指令开销（相较存储映射方式节省5指令/5周期）；归档配置最坏情况91周期，集成配置43周期；相对20ms执行帧裕度分别约7300倍和15000倍。

Conclusion: 专用化的价值在于降低指令计数和简化软件，而非保证确定性；零指令传感器读取与乘法器无关，是独立于其他扩展的设计选择。

Abstract: We present a case study in application-driven specialization of a five-stage soft processor, evaluated on the inner control loop of a reaction-wheel self-balancing bicycle. Starting from a custom 32-bit RISC core in the MIPS tradition, we specialize the design in two ways. First, two frequently accessed peripheral inputs are mapped directly into architectural register state, written every cycle by hardware and owned exclusively through the register file's write-port structure rather than by arbitration. Second, four periodic PWM channels are offloaded to hardware and driven continuously from four exported registers, removing periodic actuation from software entirely. Because peripheral values are addressable as ordinary register operands, all ten sensor reads in the control loop cost no dedicated instruction and no dedicated cycle, folding into arithmetic that executes anyway; the memory-mapped equivalent requires an explicit load per snapshot and costs five extra instructions and cycles. The actuation path likewise removes waveform maintenance from software entirely. We report two configurations, because the extensions and the single-cycle array multiplier they were deployed alongside are not present together in a single archived build: an archived configuration, whose worst-case loop is 91 cycles, and the integrated configuration matching the deployed system, at 43 cycles. Against a 20 ms actuation frame these are margins of roughly 7,300x and 15,000x. The deadline is met by so wide a margin in either case that the specialization was not necessary for real-time compliance; its value lies in instruction count and software simplicity, not in determinism, which an on-chip single-cycle I/O region already provides. The zero-instruction sensor read is independent of that choice: the multiplier cannot affect whether a peripheral read needs an instruction of its own.

</details>


### [111] [An Open-Source Power Measurement Platform for System-Level Semiconductor Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05888)
*Linus Bantel,Sarah Rottacker,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Raspberry Pi的紧凑型硬件平台，用于嵌入式设备的自动化系统级功耗测量，通过HTTP接口实现远程固件上传与测量结果获取，支持自动化基准测试与可复现实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代嵌入式与半导体系统的能效评估和压力测试需要精确的功耗测量，但工业半导体测试设备往往价格昂贵且难以集成到灵活的实验流程中，因此亟需一种低成本、易集成且可扩展的功耗测量解决方案。

Method: 构建一个集成了Raspberry Pi控制器、高精度电流测量设备和微控制器被测设备（DUT）的硬件平台，实现自动化固件部署、同步设备执行和高分辨率电流采集，并提供轻量级HTTP接口用于远程上传固件和获取测量结果。

Result: 成功实现了一个紧凑且可扩展的功耗测量平台，支持自动化基准测试和跨固件变体的可复现实验，适用于固件能耗分析和回归测试等应用场景。

Conclusion: 该平台采用易获取的硬件组件和开源软件工具实现，成本低、可扩展性强，适合研究、原型开发和教育环境，为嵌入式设备的能耗评估提供了一种可行且易于推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate power measurement is not only essential for evaluating the energy efficiency of modern embedded and semiconductor systems, but power draw is an important proxy during stress testing. Industrial semiconductor test equipment, however, is often expensive and difficult to integrate into flexible experimental workflows.
  This paper presents a compact and extensible hardware platform for automated system-level power measurement of embedded devices. The proposed setup integrates a Raspberry Pi controller, a precision current measurement device, and a microcontroller-based Device-under-Test (DUT). The system supports automated firmware deployment, synchronized device execution, and high-resolution current acquisition.
  A lightweight HTTP-based interface allows remote clients to upload firmware binaries and retrieve measurement results. This design enables automated benchmarking and reproducible experiments across multiple firmware variants.
  The platform is implemented using accessible hardware components and open-source software tools, making it suitable for research, prototyping, and educational environments. We describe the architecture of the platform, the automated testing workflow, and potential application scenarios, including firmware energy profiling and regression testing.

</details>


### [112] [Automated Synthesis of Heterogeneous, Hierarchical, Scoped Coherence Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05965)
*Fletch Rydell,An Qi Zhang,Nicolai Oswald,Andres Goens,Vijay Nagarajan,Daniel Sorin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ShimGen，一个自动化合成引擎，通过新颖的shim API抽象来生成层次化缓存一致性协议的接口，避免了手工设计的错误，同时支持比传统SWMR假设更宽松的访问模式以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代处理器设计趋向于异构、层次化的缓存一致性共享内存模型（如CXL、AMBA CHI），需要连接不同层级的一致性协议。手工设计协议间的接口(shim)容易出错且复杂；而现有的自动合成方法为保证正确性做出了过于保守的假设（如SWMR），牺牲了性能。

Method: 提出了一种shim API——一个通用抽象层，用于按语义一致性保证对协议事务进行分类。基于此API构建了自动化合成引擎ShimGen，能够合成包含SWMR和宽松访问的协议，并利用现代架构优化如作用域内存访问和惰性失效。

Result: 通过两个案例研究验证：(1) 与gem5中AMD APU的手工设计协议对比，ShimGen生成的协议相似，但在特定场景下修正了手工协议无法满足复合一致性的缺陷；(2) 对比分析表明，支持非SWMR访问的全局协议比严格的SWMR协议具有更好的性能表现。

Conclusion: ShimGen成功实现了层次化一致性协议接口的自动化合成，在保证正确性的同时突破了传统SWMR假设的性能限制，为异构层次化系统提供了可靠且高效的协议合成方案。

Abstract: Processor design is converging on a new model of cache-coherent shared memory characterized by heterogeneity, hierarchy, and scopes. Protocols like CXL or AMBA CHI are used as global protocols to combine multiple clusters, each with its own cluster-level coherence protocols. Manually designing shims to interface between these cluster and global protocols is subtle and error-prone. Automatic synthesis, on the other hand, makes simplifying assumptions like the single-writer multiple-reader (SWMR) invariant, that sacrifice performance for simplicity and guaranteed correctness.
  We present a shim API \textemdash a generic abstraction that enables us to classify protocol transactions by their semantic coherence guarantees. Our automated synthesis engine, ShimGen, uses this shim API to automatically compose protocols with both SWMR and relaxed accesses, taking advantage of modern architectural optimizations like scoped memory accesses and lazy invalidation. We demonstrate ShimGen's efficacy on two case studies. First, we compare its output to a manually-designed hierarchical protocol for the AMD APU released in gem5. ShimGen's output is similar to the existing protocol. However, we identify one scenario where the manually-designed protocol fails to uphold compound consistency, while ShimGen's does. Second, we compare the performance of a hierarchical protocol with a global protocol that exploits non-SWMR accesses to a hierarchical protocol with a strictly SWMR global protocol. The result shows the performance benefits of accommodating global protocols with non-SWMR behavior.

</details>


### [113] [Breaking Memory Bottlenecks in Quantum Control Systems for More Precise Experiments and Higher Throughput Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06318)
*Yicheng Guang,Neel Vora,Yilun Xu,Yueqi Chen,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Ant-Q，一种集成DRAM和BRAM的量子控制存储层次设计，解决存储瓶颈问题，将电路加载和读出开销从22.90%-1417.05%降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算的发展，需要更精确的实验和更高的电路吞吐量来推进量子器件开发并验证更多领域应用。然而，量子控制系统层存在存储瓶颈，源于有限的片上BRAM容量和DRAM的非确定性延迟，阻碍了这一需求的实现。

Method: 提出Ant-Q存储层次设计，将DRAM与BRAM集成，以支持流水线量子电路执行，同时确保确定性的电路间时序特性。

Result: 在26个真实世界实验和计算电路上评估Ant-Q，结果表明：支持用于1Q和2Q随机基准测试的深层电路；将电路加载和读出上传相对于执行时间的开销从22.90%-1417.05%降低至接近零。Ant-Q正集成到QubiC 3.0中，部分功能已可用。

Conclusion: Ant-Q有效突破了量子控制系统的存储瓶颈，显著降低了电路加载和读出开销，实现了流水线量子电路执行与确定性时序保证，为量子计算实验精度提升和吞吐量改进提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As quantum computing continues to demonstrate promise and attract growing attention, there is an increasing need for more precise experiments to advance the development of quantum devices, as well as higher circuit throughput to validate more domain applications. However, this need is hindered by a memory bottleneck at the quantum control system layer, arising from limited on-chip BRAM capacity and the non-deterministic latency of DRAM. To break this bottleneck, we present Ant-Q, a memory hierarchy design that integrates DRAM with BRAM to support pipelined quantum circuit execution while ensuring deterministic inter-circuit timing. We evaluated Ant-Q using 26 real-world experimental and computing circuits. The results show that Ant-Q supports deep circuits for 1Q and 2Q Randomized Benchmarking and reduces the overhead of circuit loading and readout uplink relative to execution time from 22.90%-1417.05% to near zero. Ant-Q is being integrated into QubiC 3.0, with part of its functionalities already available.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Timestep-Conditioned Transformers for Global Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06241)
*Sam Levang,Fran Bartolic,Ty Dickinson,Chase Dwelle,Paulius Rauba,Viktor Cikojevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-3是一个概率性全球天气模型，通过多时间步长推理解决了固定自回归时间步长的权衡问题，使用单一权重集实现了近SOTA的中期概率预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型依赖预定的固定自回归时间步长，存在基本权衡：短时间步长（1-6小时）能精细分辨日周期内的大气动力学但在给定预测范围内增加误差累积，而长时间步长（24小时）减少误差累积但限制了短期预测的可用性。

Method: 提出了GEM-3概率性全球天气模型，采用显式多时间步长推理方法，在推理时可配置模型时间步长以平衡广泛预测范围内的可预测性和可用性；使用混合时间步长训练；模型为轻量级邻域注意力变换器，约134M参数，采用等矩形网格。

Result: 单一权重集可在推理时配置时间步长；混合时间步长训练相比时间步长专用模型持续改善rollout稳定性；实现了近SOTA的中期概率预测能力、稳定的扩展范围rollout、高效的训练和推理以及决策相关诊断。

Conclusion: GEM-3成功解决了天气预报模型时间步长选择的基本权衡问题，提供了一个实用的预测系统，在单一模型框架下实现了优异的预测性能和灵活性。

Abstract: Existing machine-learning weather forecasting models rely on predetermined and fixed autoregressive timesteps. The choice of model timestep involves a fundamental trade-off: shorter timesteps (e.g. 1 to 6 hours) finely resolve atmospheric dynamics within the diurnal cycle but increase error accumulation for a given forecast horizon, while longer timesteps (e.g. 24 hours) reduce error accumulation but limit the usability of short-range forecasts where sub-daily predictability is high. In this work, we present GEM-3, a probabilistic global weather model that addresses this trade-off through explicit multi-timestep inference. With a single set of trained weights, the model timestep can be configured at inference time to balance predictability and usability across a broad forecast horizon. Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [115] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MS-MLB，这是首个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的多发性硬化症与健康对照分类的开放基准测试平台，采用泄漏控制的评估管道，Gradient Boosting模型在保留集上取得了93.83的MS研究评分和0.989的AUC-ROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化症的诊断依赖于临床评估、MRI和实验室检查，虽然血液RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关的免疫信号，但缺乏一个可重复的、开放基准测试平台来评估机器学习方法在MS血液转录组数据分类中的性能。现有研究虽已应用机器学习于MS血液转录组数据，但缺少标准化的比较框架。

Method: 构建了MS-MLB基准测试框架，使用公开的GSE17048队列数据，将其转换为MS与健康对照分类任务。采用泄漏控制的共享评估管道，包括嵌套交叉验证、分层保留集、Bootstrap置信区间、ROC和精确率-召回率分析、校准测量以及探索性的MS研究评分。框架支持研究者无需重新配置即可重新运行评估，并包含外部模型提交途径。

Result: 在最终基准测试总结中，Gradient Boosting算法在保留集上排名第一，MS研究评分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，敏感性为0.950，特异性为0.778，F1分数为0.927，Brier分数为0.050。该评分仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个专注于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的MS与健康对照分类的开放基准，提供了文档化的外部模型提交路径。该基准为机器学习方法的比较研究提供了可重复的评估框架，促进了该领域的标准化研究，但需要强调其评分未经过临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [116] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTER通过挖掘冻结预测器残差的解释性特征来驱动轻量级事后校正器，在六个数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上超越所有专用特征工程系统，将最弱骨干网络的误差降低高达27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器通常以结构化、重复的方式失败，通过微调修复成本高昂。现有自动特征工程建模数据生成过程，而校正特征应建模模型失败过程，需要一种轻量级、源无关的特征发现方法来系统性地改进冻结预测器。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网络冻结，使用两个互补生成器挖掘残差：原始输入通道的组合搜索，以及大语言模型提出的命名特征组合、二进制标志和可执行代码。验证接地门控接受或拒绝每个候选特征，验证选择的校正器应用接受的特征或保持预测不变。该流程允许在相同条件下评估不同特征工程系统。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在每个特征预算下都超越所有专用特征工程系统，将校正器单独实现的改进大致翻倍，将最弱骨干网络的误差降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端间具有鲁棒性，即使在微调骨干网络之上应用也能保持。

Conclusion: CRAFTER证明了通过建模模型失败过程而非数据生成过程来发现校正特征的有效性，为冻结预测器提供了一种高效、可解释的改进方法，同时为归因预测改进到特征源提供了评估工具。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [117] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [118] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CGTime，一个40亿参数的计算引导时间序列-语言模型，通过解耦感知与描述解决了现有方法中的自监督陷阱问题，在多变量理解任务上超越了更大的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个关键问题：1）自监督陷阱——使用LLM生成时间序列描述作为训练标签，导致标签质量受限于模型本身要学习的感知能力；2）多变量数据缺口——现有数据集多为单变量，而重要模式（跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）仅出现在多变量场景，这正是标注LLM的局限所在。这两个问题形成三难困境：现有方法只能兼顾可靠性、真实性和可扩展性中的两项。

Method: 提出解耦感知与描述的方案：确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算统计特征，LLM仅负责将这些预计算的事实转化为自然语言描述。感知任务（LLM表现较差的部分）由计算完成，而表达任务由LLM承担。基于此构建了CGTime模型（4B参数），实现了计算引导的训练范式。

Result: CGTime在多变量理解任务上显著优于更大的通用模型：在保留基准测试中取得最佳多变量事实得分（0.283，相比GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），该优势在Holm校正配对显著性检验中对所有基线均成立。模型在生成标题中陈述可验证数值事实的准确性更高，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过将感知（计算统计特征）与描述（自然语言生成）解耦，成功规避了自监督陷阱，使模型能够学习到超越标注者认知的知识。CGTime证明了在多变量时间序列理解任务中，这种计算引导的方法能够在保持可扩展性的同时实现更高的准确性和可靠性，为时间序列-语言模型的发展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [119] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiSR框架，通过显式解耦3D感知与推理过程，利用现有感知模型重建3D几何证据，并用LoRA微调LLM进行推理，在空间推理基准测试中取得了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过大规模训练联合获取隐式3D感知和推理能力，但本文观察到现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而大语言模型在组合和符号推理方面表现出色。这种互补优势促使作者探索将3D感知与推理显式解耦的替代范式。

Method: 提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)框架：(1)利用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据；(2)使用LoRA微调LLM，使其仅基于显式几何证据进行推理。该方法无需大规模3D视觉问答训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: DiSR在主流空间推理基准测试中达到了具有竞争力的性能，同时展现出更好的可解释性、模块化特性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是端到端建模的有效替代范式，为空间智能提供了一种可扩展且有效的解决方案，证明了解耦方法在空间推理任务中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [120] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了因子化生成模型中一个关键缺陷：仅匹配边缘分布无法保证风格变量与类别标签的独立性，即使边缘分布看似完美，类别信息仍可能泄露在潜在表示中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型通常通过将潜在风格变量的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验来正则化，并据此声称风格表示与类别信息独立。然而作者发现这种解释存在根本性错误——仅匹配边缘分布并不能约束类别条件分布，导致潜在风格变量在边缘分布完美的情况下仍可高度预测类别标签。

Method: 作者推导了一个精确分解，证明这种不匹配是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一，并指出消除该问题是必要但不充分条件。实证研究中，作者测试了一个案例模型和四个代表性潜在基线，采用六种独立扰动（涉及模型容量、课程学习、先验几何和监督）以及四种缓解策略进行系统性实验。

Result: 所有测试模型在边缘分布上达到接近零的全局MMD，但线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（随机基线为10%）。作者的模型达到99.15%的聚类准确率，但外部评估的类别条件生成成功率仅为16%。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。事后条件先验在MNIST上将外部类别生成提升至0.97，但在CIFAR-10上仅为0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布计算的散度无法证明其与类别标签的独立性，报告边缘统计量本身并不能验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的因子化性质。这一发现对现有因子化生成模型的评估范式提出了根本性质疑。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [121] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将对抗流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络，解决了原有约束在密度估计中的问题，并在多个博弈环境中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注强化学习与反事实遗憾最小化的结合以平衡计算速度与性能，但对不完全信息博弈中的生成式采样框架研究较少。本研究旨在填补这一空白，将生成式流网络框架扩展至不完全信息博弈场景。

Method: 提出了信息流网络，这是对抗流网络在不完全信息博弈上的扩展。证明了完整信息博弈中原有的生成式流网络约束在不完全信息博弈中会导致无效的密度估计与训练目标，并设计了新的约束来解决该问题，使IFlowNets成为AFlowNets的严格泛化。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验中，IFlowNets在性能与速度上与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret Minimization（OSMCCFR）和标准强化学习方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: 信息流网络成功将生成式流网络扩展至不完全信息博弈领域，解决了理论与实际应用中的关键问题，为博弈论应用提供了一种新的有效方法，具有进一步研究的价值。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [122] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过结构化规则监督解决多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令跟随问题，仅用10K样本将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的严格准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态指令通常包含多个重要性不等的要求，而现有训练数据仍将指令跟随简化为回答单一问题。作者通过"规则理解"视角研究这一差距：将模型定位为执行者而非生成者，使其根据优先级规则集进行结构化验证。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：1)生成人格-任务对；2)前缀引导规则集；3)质量过滤的规则集；4)结构化验证轨迹。同时引入PRISM-Eval评估基准，包含Loose和Strict两种基于确定性匹配的指标，无需推理时的裁判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的严格准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能。该方法在四个额外的开源MLLM上（包括密集和MoE架构）均取得增益，表明结构化规则监督的有效性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 结构化规则监督是实现多规则、优先级感知多模态指令跟随的可扩展路径，为缩小训练数据与真实世界指令复杂度差距提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [123] [Hybrid-Adaptive Thread Tuning to Mitigate Simulation Execution Bottlenecks in High-Performance Reinforcement Learning Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06025)
*Jiming Su,Hantao Hua,Lujia Yin,Yiping Yao,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoThread，一种用于强化学习推理中仿真瓶颈缓解的混合自适应线程调优方法。通过物理信息神经算子（PINO）和有限源M/M/1排队模型，实现了动态工作负载下的快速准确线程数预测，平均加速比提升18.4%，执行时间最多减少83.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真在环决策系统中，强化学习推理受到仿真器端执行开销的制约。现有线程策略难以在执行前或执行期间匹配线程资源，导致资源竞争、调度开销和吞吐量降低。通过实证分析发现，任务执行时间与调度时间的比值是决定最优线程数的关键因素。

Method: 提出AutoThread混合自适应线程调优方法：1）使用物理信息神经算子（PINO）作为线程数预测器；2）引入有限源M/M/1排队模型来约束和引导预测，实现动态工作负载下的快速准确估计；3）执行负载感知的在线微调以补偿预测误差并优化资源分配。

Result: 实验结果表明：相比静态策略平均加速比提升18.4%；相比XGBoost和Reinforcer分别实现1.7倍和1.8倍的平均吞吐量；与最先进方法相比，执行时间最多减少83.8%。

Conclusion: AutoThread有效解决了仿真在环强化学习推理中的线程资源匹配问题，通过结合物理信息神经网络和排队理论模型，在动态工作负载下实现了高效的线程调优，显著提升了系统性能和资源利用率。

Abstract: In simulation-in-the-loop decision-making systems, reinforcement learning (RL) inference is often constrained by simulator-side execution overhead, where workloads are highly dynamic and sensitive to runtime thread configurations. Existing multithreaded strategies struggle to match thread resources before or during execution, causing resource contention, scheduling overhead, and reduced throughput. Through empirical analysis, we identify the ratio of task execution time to scheduling time as the key factor determining the optimal thread count. Building on this insight, we propose AutoThread, a hybrid adaptive thread-tuning method for mitigating simulation bottlenecks in RL inference. AutoThread employs a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) as a thread-count predictor and incorporates a finite-source M/M/1 queueing model to constrain and guide prediction, enabling fast and accurate estimation under dynamic workloads. It further performs load-aware online fine-tuning to compensate for prediction errors and refine resource allocation. Experiments show that AutoThread improves average speedup by 18.4\% over static strategies, achieves average throughput of 1.7x and 1.8x that of XGBoost and Reinforcer, respectively, and reduces execution time by up to 83.8\% compared with state-of-the-art methods. Our code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/suchenjm/AutoThread.

</details>


### [124] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DG-FedReuse通过选择性重用年龄衰减的缓存更新，在90轮联邦学习中将上行链路数据传输量节省83.36-85.42%（相比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%），准确率损失控制在-5.29至-0.14个百分点范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临两个主要的通信开销来源：本地优化过程和模型更新传输。现有方法如Top-K压缩虽然能减少传输量，但仍需每轮传输新鲜更新，未能充分挖掘历史更新的潜在价值。研究动机在于探索如何通过重用历史缓存更新来进一步降低通信开销，同时保持模型性能。

Method: 提出DG-FedReuse机制，核心包含四个组件：(1)随机头梯度差异代理：用于评估缓存更新的可用性；(2)轮次依赖阈值：控制何时可使用缓存更新；(3)硬缓存年龄限制和新鲜客户端配额：约束重用行为，确保足够的新鲜更新参与训练；(4)自适应逐张量Top-K数值域表示：用于压缩新鲜更新。系统在满足梯度差异阈值条件下，选择性让客户端贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新。

Result: 在六个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性(α=0.5)设置下，90轮训练预算内，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的上行链路节省，相比匹配的Top-K FedAvg(76.88%)有显著提升；种子对齐的准确率差异在-5.29至-0.14个百分点范围内。最佳测试准确率相对FedAvg在-2.38至+0.45个百分点。当考虑对称密集模型下行链路敏感性时，总节省降至41.68-42.71%，相比Top-K FedAvg的增量增益为3.24-4.27个百分点，说明通信节省结论依赖于计算边界设定。

Conclusion: 研究证明了缓存更新重用机制在模拟器层面的可行性，能够显著减少上行链路通信量且对准确率影响可控。然而，研究明确指出了其局限性：仅在模拟器中验证，未建立无偏泛化性、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能耗节省、更快收敛性，也未证明相比现有陈旧更新和惰性聚合方法的优越性。通信节省程度严重依赖于计算边界定义（是否包含下行链路）。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [125] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT提出了一种参数高效的多任务监督微调框架，通过可合并的适配器状态替代全模型检查点，在保持性能的同时显著降低了存储和部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务监督微调(SFT)中不同任务在不同时间达到最佳泛化性能，而msft方法通过任务级回滚解决此问题，但其原始实现需要存储和恢复完整的模型检查点，导致阶段转换的存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器，在任务级峰值处回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余活动混合上训练。在层级上，每个适配器将AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层应用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，保持更新对输入线性、秩有界且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干比较协议下，AuroSFT在五个骨干网络上均获得更高准确率，平均准确率达到61.36%，而对应的msft参考行为59.85%。

Conclusion: AuroSFT成功地将过拟合感知的多任务SFT的携带状态重新表述为紧凑、可合并的适配器状态，在保持或提升性能的同时实现了参数高效的训练。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [126] [Does Latent Context Help? A Controlled Evaluation of Inverse Reinforcement Learning in Arctic Shipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06105)
*Vaishnav Vaidheeswaran,Dilith Jayakody,Biruk Ambaw,Jaswanth Kumar,Md Mahbub Alam,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对北极航运数据的控制实验发现，现有元逆强化学习模型中的潜在上下文变量并未真正恢复隐藏的船舶偏好，而是重新编码了观察状态中已有的信息；研究建议在添加潜在上下文之前，应先测试观察到的航线、环境和船舶特征是否已经能够解释行为变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能辅助导航可以帮助北极航运适应快速变化的海冰条件，但可靠部署需要可解释且对环境变化具有鲁棒性的奖励模型。逆强化学习为从船舶轨迹中恢复奖励提供了框架，最近的元逆强化学习方法引入了潜在上下文变量来捕捉行为异质性。然而，目前尚不清楚这些潜在表示是否真正恢复了隐藏偏好，或者仅仅重新编码了观察状态中已有的信息。

Method: 研究对来自9个北极航运季节中202艘船舶的3,186次AIS航行轨迹进行了控制评估，比较了线性共享奖励、非线性共享奖励和基于相同非线性架构的潜在上下文模型。采用行为分析、上下文探测和预注册的特征隐藏消融实验来分析模型性能。

Result: 非线性奖励比线性基线提高了50.9%的保留似然，而添加船舶特定潜在上下文反而使性能降低16.5%。行为分析、上下文探测和预注册的特征隐藏消融实验表明，明显的船舶层面变异很大程度上由可观察的航线和环境条件解释，而非隐藏的船舶特定因素。此外，预测准确性、航线保真度和奖励迁移产生了不同的模型排名，表明没有任何单一指标足以评估学习到的奖励。

Conclusion: 这些发现促使在添加每船潜在上下文之前，测试观察到的航线、环境和船舶特征是否已经解释了行为变异。这支持在安全关键领域进行更可信的AI部署。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-assisted navigation can help Arctic shipping adapt to rapidly changing sea-ice conditions, but reliable deployment requires reward models that are interpretable and robust to changing environments. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a framework for recovering such rewards from vessel trajectories, while recent meta-IRL methods introduce latent context variables to capture behavioral heterogeneity. However, it remains unclear whether these latent representations recover genuinely hidden preferences or simply re-encode information already available in the observed state. We conduct a controlled evaluation on 3,186 AIS-derived voyages from 202 vessels across nine Arctic shipping seasons, comparing a linear shared reward, a nonlinear shared reward, and a latent-context model built on the same nonlinear architecture. The nonlinear reward improves held-out likelihood by 50.9% over the linear baseline, whereas adding vessel-specific latent context reduces performance by 16.5%. Behavioral analysis, context probes, and a pre-registered feature-hiding ablation show that apparent vessel-level variation is largely explained by observable route and environmental conditions rather than hidden vessel-specific factors. Moreover, predictive accuracy, route fidelity, and reward transfer yield different model rankings, demonstrating that no single metric is sufficient to evaluate learned rewards. These findings motivate testing whether the observed route, environmental, and vessel features already explain behavioral variation before adding per-vessel latent context. This supports more trustworthy AI deployment in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AuroOFT方法，通过添加零起始门控低秩非线性残差来增强量化正交微调(QOFT)，在保持量化兼容性的同时引入非线性校正能力，显著提升了语言模型适配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化正交微调(QOFT)方法虽然能实现参数高效的低比特语言模型适配，但其任务特定更新被限制为线性正交变换，缺乏输入依赖的非线性校正能力，这限制了模型的适应性和性能表现。

Method: AuroOFT将QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支，在每个适配的线性层上附加零起始门控低秩非线性残差。具体包括：1)将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜在空间；2)使用自适应非线性基配合有界或令牌依赖的门控机制；3)零初始化的上投影使AuroOFT在初始化时功能上与QOFT相同；4)正交性保持在分支级别的稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析器协议下，AuroOFT在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置上将Macro-6指标相比匹配的QOFT提升了1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并且在代表性规模下相比QLoRA节省了32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT成功地在保持量化兼容性和稳定性的同时引入了非线性校正能力，实现了参数效率与性能提升的双重目标，为低比特语言模型的高效适配提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [128] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习排序的框架，用于在GPU上为张量网络收缩选择高效的收缩计划，通过结构特征表示和梯度提升模型来预测性能，从而减少经典量子电路模拟的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟对量子算法的开发和验证至关重要，但成本随电路规模快速增长。张量网络收缩可以通过利用电路结构来降低成本，但其效率高度依赖于所选的收缩计划。在GPU上，具有相似理论复杂度的计划可能表现迥异，因为执行还取决于并行性、归约结构、内存流量和收缩几何等因素。现有的理论复杂度指标无法准确预测实际GPU执行性能。

Method: 提出了一种学习排序框架，用于在执行收缩计划前进行选择。每个计划通过直接从成对收缩序列导出的结构特征进行表示，并使用列表式和成对目标函数从GPU测量数据中训练梯度提升排序器。在多种电路族上评估模型，使用独立的分布内测试集和电路族偏移测试集，并与随机基线和MinFill基线进行比较。

Result: 学习到的排序器通常能识别更好的计划，其中列表式模型提供了最强的整体决策质量。研究还比较了两种GPU架构上的经验计划排序，并评估了源训练模型在第二个设备上的表现。排序在GPU间保持大部分稳定（虽不完全稳定），模型保留了有用的决策质量。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将学习排序作为减少收缩计划搜索的实用方法，同时表明性能仍然部分依赖于后端。这为量子电路经典模拟的优化提供了一种新的实用方法，能够在GPU上更高效地选择收缩计划。

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size. Tensor-network contraction can reduce this cost by exploiting circuit structure, although its efficiency depends strongly on the chosen contraction plan. On GPUs, plans with similar theoretical complexity may perform very differently because execution also depends on parallelism, reduction structure, memory traffic, and contraction geometry. We present a learning-to-rank framework for selecting efficient contraction plans before executing them. Each plan is represented by structural features derived directly from its sequence of pairwise contractions, and gradient-boosted rankers are trained from GPU measurements using listwise and pairwise objectives. We evaluate the resulting models on diverse circuit families, using separate in-distribution and circuit-family-shift test sets, and compare them with random and MinFill-based baselines. The learned rankers generally identify better plans, with the listwise model providing the strongest overall decision quality. We also study backend shift by comparing empirical plan orderings on two GPU architectures and evaluating the source-trained models on the second device without retraining. The rankings remain substantially, though not perfectly, stable across GPUs, and the models retain useful decision quality. These results support Learning to Rank as a practical way to reduce contraction-plan search, while showing that performance remains partly backend dependent.

</details>


### [129] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向零售投资者的智能投顾系统，通过三阶段强化学习框架（自监督跨资产编码器、混合专家分配策略、LoRA个性化适配器）实现个性化、税务感知的投资组合管理，并完成了端到端集成测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售投资者缺乏机构客户所享有的个性化、税务感知的投资组合管理服务。现有智能投顾采用静态、基于规则的配置方式，而机构级系统则需要最低账户金额和技术栈，个人投资者无法获得。

Method: 构建了一个FastAPI后端和Web仪表板系统，用户可用自然语言描述投资目标，系统将其路由至六种投资指令之一，并通过三阶段强化学习系统生成实时、经纪商集成的投资组合建议：(1)自监督跨资产编码器；(2)带有学习意图路由器的混合专家分配策略；(3)轻量级LoRA适配器，从个人经纪行为中学习个性化推荐，无需重训练共享模型。

Result: 系统已完成功能开发和端到端集成测试（对接Alpaca经纪商API，纸交易模式），包含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作链。进行了14天滚动回测验证（含bootstrap置信区间），但尚未对真实终端用户开放。

Conclusion: 报告了多个工程实践经验教训——静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题、端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据——这些经验可推广至其他基于外部实时数据源的应用强化学习系统。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [130] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型在野火后泥石流预测中的表现，发现TabPFN模型性能最佳，识别出短时降雨强度和风暴累积量为关键预测因子，并通过合成数据增强有效提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测对于在强降雨期间保护社区、基础设施和资源至关重要，但现有预测面临特征空间中泥石流与非泥石流事件重叠、模型可解释性需求和训练数据有限等挑战。

Method: 研究采用美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流事件观测数据，系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN），使用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能，通过SHAP方法分析特征重要性，并采用合成数据增强技术解决数据稀缺问题。

Result: TabPFN模型在未增强数据情况下表现最佳，威胁得分为0.637；SHAP分析显示短时降雨强度和风暴累积量是最高排名特征，而燃烧严重程度和地形特征贡献较小；合成数据增强使除CNN外的所有模型性能提升，深度学习模型平均威胁得分提升+0.041。

Conclusion: 研究通过结合严格的模型基准测试、可解释特征分析和合成数据增强，为改善野火后泥石流预测提供了综合框架，为灾害预警和风险管理提供了可靠的方法论基础。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [131] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OSPDIM框架，一种用于在线脑机接口的无监督域适应方法，解决了标准黎曼对齐方法在类别不平衡时存在的几何错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于黎曼中心化变换(RCT)的对齐方法隐式假设类别先验分布平衡，但实际在线BCI场景中标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术在几何上错误地对齐目标数据分布。

Method: 提出OSPDIM（在线SPD流形信息最大化）框架，在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，通过信息最大化优化来纠正由不平衡数据流引起的几何偏斜，并在线估计和纠正几何偏差，而非依赖全局批统计量的离线方法。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵上的仿真可视化了OSPDIM成功纠正了标准中心化失败时的错位问题；在多个运动想象数据集上的广泛实验表明，OSPDIM显著优于标准黎曼基线方法，特别是在具有严重类别不平衡的挑战性在线适应场景中。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实用的即插即用BCI系统提供了鲁棒的解决方案，能够有效处理在线场景中的标签偏移问题，在具有严重类别不平衡的情况下仍能保持良好的适应性能。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [132] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案，通过可恢复驱逐替代传统的二态保留/删除策略，在保持内存效率的同时显著提升了长上下文任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存驱逐策略存在关键缺陷：基于注意力分数驱逐不重要token的决策是不可逆的，一旦驱逐就无法恢复。然而在解码过程中，随着生成查询的演变，token和窗口的重要性会发生变化，导致标准驱逐策略永久丢弃了在全缓存模型下后续会获得大量注意力的状态。

Method: 提出QEvict三层KV缓存管理方案：(1)高置信度窗口以全精度保留；(2)中等置信度窗口存储在量化的可恢复层；(3)仅删除最低置信度窗口。解码过程中，累积注意力分数动态更新窗口重要性，当量化窗口重新变得重要时，被反量化并提升至全精度层，实现可恢复驱逐机制。

Result: 在固定内存预算下，QEvict在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准测试中持续优于代表性驱逐和量化基线方法，有效减少了注意力缺失并改善了信息保留能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入Future Missed Mass和Global LIR诊断指标揭示了现有驱逐策略的局限性，QEvict的可恢复驱逐设计成功解决了重要性漂移问题，在保持历史上下文广度的同时保留重要区域的精确全精度，为KV缓存管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [133] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了量子结构世界模型(QSWMs)，将量子理论启发的数学结构引入世界建模，发现复值QSWMs具有有前景的局部预测潜力，但在长期推演方面存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐藏状态或Transformer激活来表示状态，作者探索量子理论启发的数学结构（如复值表示和密度矩阵形式的隐变量）是否能作为有用的归纳偏置，从而改进世界模型的预测能力。

Method: 提出量子结构世界模型框架，包含结构化隐状态、隐变量转移算子和测量启发的解码映射；建立了三个基础性质：经典包含性、预测充分性和结构紧凑性；实例化了复值和密度矩阵形式的QSWM变体。

Result: 在基本细胞自动机任务上评估，复值QSWMs展现出有前景的局部预测潜力，但在长期推演方面存在局限；密度矩阵变体也显示出一定的限制。

Conclusion: 量子理论启发的结构在世界建模中具有潜力，特别是复值表示对局部预测有益，但该方法在长期推演方面仍需改进。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [134] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过凸优化学习和蒸馏过程，将非线性动力系统转化为紧凑线性状态空间表示的方法，并提供了首个端到端的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接求解非凸系统辨识问题来学习非线性动力系统非常困难。研究动机是能否不直接求解非凸问题，而是通过紧凑的线性状态空间表示来学习非线性动力系统，并提供理论保证。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段：(1) 使用观测谱滤波(OSF)方法学习隐式谱预测器，这是一种凸方法，能与系统的最佳线性观测器竞争；(2) 应用谱到LDS蒸馏技术，将该预测器转换为显式的循环线性动力系统。整个过程避免了直接求解非凸优化问题。

Result: 主要定理表明，蒸馏得到的LDS的平均预测误差可分解为指数级小的蒸馏项和由最佳观测器的Luenberger复杂度决定的OSF学习项。该保证是无维度依赖的，取决于观测器复杂度而非表示非线性系统所需的潜在维度。实验表明，在线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务中，该方法产生的紧凑LDS预测器匹配或优于直接训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 这是首个端到端可证明的方法，能够通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏过程，提取非线性动力系统的最优LDS表示。该方法为非线性系统的线性化表示学习提供了理论保证和实用工具。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [135] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在模型收敛后通过固定扰动来识别受虚假相关性影响样本的方法，无需组标签或早停，提升了最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化在包含虚假相关性的数据上训练的模型虽然在平均准确率上表现很好，但在相关性不成立的子群体上表现较差。现有识别受影响样本的方法依赖早期训练信号，需要选择干预时机（通常需要组标签验证集）。作者发现在模型收敛后仍存在可利用的信号，无需早停或组标签。

Method: 作者利用模型收敛后的行为差异：符合虚假相关性的样本被共享规则分类，其余样本通过输入特定配置拟合，后者对输入扰动更脆弱。通过向收敛模型输入施加固定扰动，发现不符合虚假相关性的样本预测更容易被翻转，从而识别出受影响样本。该方法仅需对每训练样本做两次前向传播，无需任何阶段的组标注，也不需要早停时机选择。利用检测到的样本对训练进行重平衡。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实组标签的85.8%。方法仅需两次前向传播，无需组标签和早停参数。

Conclusion: 证明了模型收敛后存在可用于识别虚假相关性样本的信号，提出了一种简单高效的方法，显著降低了依赖虚假相关性方法对组标注和早停的超参数依赖，在实际应用中具有更好的可操作性。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [136] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明探索性建模(XM)的"第三预训练轴"实际上是"自由度"(freedom)，即模型行为约束的弱度。作者从理论和实验两方面证明XM通过增加输出数量K来优化自由度，而非生成表达性，且基于自由度的选择方法在30个案例中29次胜过验证集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成训练中，每轮只产生一个输出，导致模型倾向于收敛到常见答案而限制了表达能力。探索性建模(XM)通过每轮产生K个输出来解决此问题，声称"探索性"是第三个预训练轴。作者想要揭示这个第三轴的本质到底是什么。

Method: 1. 理论推导：证明XM的平均损失取决于候选者错过可接受区域的概率，探索将错过概率提升到K次方，当K>1时匹配概率随自由度增加；2. 前向XM实验：测试不同K值对测得自由度的影响；3. 候选池实验：对比验证集选择与自由度选择器（读取无标签父上下文）的表现。

Result: 理论证明：对于K>1，匹配概率随自由度增加而增加。实验结果：1)较大的K值增加或饱和测得的自由度，在上下文相关目标下每个测试值都增加了自由度；2)自由度选择在30个案例中29次胜过验证选择；3)XM确实在优化自由度而非仅仅是生成表达性。

Conclusion: XM的"第三轴"本质是自由度而非生成表达性。生成表达性只是自由度的模式计数代理，丢弃了赋予自由度泛化意义的扩展结构。XM是手段，自由度是目的，基于自由度的选择在分布偏移下改善了XM性能，这为模型选择提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [137] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EvoHarness-RL方法，通过学习Harness策略让LLM代理能够有效构建和协调外部状态，在ALFWorld上达到96.9%成功率，发现训练过程中代理从频繁调用转向选择性访问（harness退火），并形成紧凑任务自适应状态基础（harness演化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期视野LLM代理依赖外部执行支持来维护状态、追踪进度、调用工具、验证结果和重用经验，但现有方法通过提示、启发式规则或领域特定约定处理状态形成和运行时控制，导致外部工作空间及其使用策略需要人工设计，缺乏系统化的策略学习机制。

Method: 提出EvoHarness-RL框架，暴露信念(Belief)、进度(Progress)和经验(Experience)作为策略面向的Harness状态；采用监督 Harness微调教导基础代理Harness动作空间及如何构建有用的外部状态；使用成本感知的GRPO探索协调策略，在长期交互中选择性读取、更新和巩固外部状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld基准上基于Qwen3-8B LLM实现96.9%成功率；发现两个关键动态：(1) Harness退火——训练将重复的Harness使用模式内化到模型策略中，代理从频繁Harness调用转向选择性外部状态访问；(2) Harness演化——进度更新和经验巩固将Harness精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基础。

Conclusion: 长期视野代理能够从可训练的外部Harness工作空间构建和协调策略中获益，这超越了简单添加更强工具或更大记忆系统的范式，为LLM代理的外部状态管理提供了新的学习框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction.
