The improvements increase for deeper models that can better exploit the enriched temporal representations learned during pre-training. These findings indicate that SPT is a simple and general strategy that enhances transformer performance on medical time-series tasks without requiring task-specific architectural changes, supporting its potential to improve robustness and accuracy in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [172] [LLM Inference Under Bursty Workload Distribution: Modifying the WAIT Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06135)
*Anjali Gangadhar Katageria,Shobha Rani,Raghu Nandan Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的WAIT算法扩展，通过在线估计请求强度来适应时变到达率，在低到达率变化场景下实现了比现有方法更高的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度算法通常假设请求服从恒定速率的泊松到达，这一假设无法反映真实流量的突发性和动态特性，导致在实际应用中性能受限。

Method: 提出对WAIT算法的轻量级扩展，基于观测到的请求间隔时间进行在线请求强度估计，无需先验流量知识即可适应时变到达率；使用MMPP合成工作负载进行仿真评估。

Result: 在低到达率变化场景下，所提方法相比Sarathi-Serve、ORCA和vLLM实现了更高的吞吐量，同时保持了相当的延迟性能。

Conclusion: 该轻量级扩展有效解决了现有调度算法对流量模式假设不合理的问题，为LLM系统在动态真实环境中的部署提供了更优的调度方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT and Claude are widely used for information retrieval and problem-solving. Recent work has focused on improving scheduling algorithms to boost throughput while maintaining low latency. However, these approaches often assume Poisson request arrivals with constant rates - an assumption that fails to reflect the inherently bursty and dynamic nature of real-world traffic. We propose a lightweight extension to the state-of-the-art WAIT algorithm [1], which adapts to time-varying arrival rates without prior traffic knowledge. The proposed algorithm performs online estimation of request intensity based on observed interarrival times. Using Markov Modulated Poisson Process (MMPP)-based synthetic workloads with diverse request types, we conduct a simulation-based evaluation demonstrating that the proposed method achieves higher throughput than Sarathi-Serve [2], ORCA [3], and vLLM [4] in the evaluated low arrival-rate shift scenarios while maintaining comparable latency.

</details>


### [173] [SkillTFM: Gated Skill Evolution for Training-Free Adaptation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06137)
*Yi He,Zhengkang Guan,Anpeng Wu,Peng Cui,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SkillTFM，一个免训练的表格基础模型适配系统，通过门控智能技能演化替代参数更新，在预测任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在实际部署中面临分布偏移、特征语义异构和任务特定模式等挑战，而现有的适配方法需要昂贵的微调或额外标注数据，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出SkillTFM系统，核心是一个可验证且可扩展的技能库，结合边界证据识别与门控技能演化：前者表征任务结构和基础模型失效模式，后者在显式验证约束下检索和扩展可复用技能。

Result: 在模拟边界设置和真实电价预测任务中，SkillTFM将AUC提升0.128-0.142，将非线性边界AUC从0.699提升至0.898；跨不同TFM骨干网络的实验验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: SkillTFM成功实现了免训练的表格基础模型适配范式转换，通过技能演化而非参数更新的方式有效应对了分布偏移和任务特异性挑战，为表格基础模型的实际部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Tabular data are ubiquitous in real-world applications and are crucial for data-driven prediction and decision-making across science, industry, finance, healthcare, and public services. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose tabular learning, offering reusable predictors across diverse datasets and substantially reducing the need for task-specific training, tuning, and model development. However, their practical deployment remains constrained by distribution shifts, heterogeneous feature semantics, and task-specific patterns that are difficult to capture without costly fine-tuning or additional labeled data.
  To this end, we propose SkillTFM, a training-free system that shifts TFM adaptation from parameter updates to the gated evolution of agentic skills. The core of SkillTFM is a verifiable and extensible skill bank that couples boundary evidence identification with gated skill evolution: the former characterizes task structure and base-model failure patterns, whereas the latter retrieves and extends reusable skills subject to explicit validation. Across simulated boundary settings and real-world electricity-price forecasting, SkillTFM improves AUC by 0.128--0.142, raises nonlinear-boundary AUC from 0.699 to 0.898. Furthermore, experiments across TFM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of SkillTFM.

</details>


### [174] [Threshold-Based Early Stopping of Accumulations in Neural Networks with Binary Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06177)
*Quentin Luquet de Saint-Germain,Massil Ait Abdeslam,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对二值神经网络的后训练早期停止机制，通过预测累加过程的最终符号来减少冗余计算，在VGG11上可减少25%的全网络算术运算，仅损失1.36个精度点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值神经网络虽然适合受限部署环境，但在二值激活情况下，每个神经元或输出通道仍需累加所有输入以确定最终符号。研究发现，累加过程中部分和往往会远离零值，使得最终符号在累加完所有项之前就已高度可预测，之后的计算虽然改变数值但不改变最终输出激活，这种计算是冗余的。

Method: 提出一种后训练早期停止机制：1) 在训练数据集上刻画运行累加的行为特征；2) 利用这些信息尽早预测最终符号；3) 一旦预测确定即停止累加。该方法不需要重新训练任何模型参数，并假设理想的权重排序来计算操作数量。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上的VGG11模型中：最深卷积层可移除86.6%的累加项，仅损失0.37个精度点；在三个最深卷积层同时使用时，可减少25%的全网络算术运算，精度损失为1.36个点。

Conclusion: 通过利用二值神经网络累加过程中的符号可预测性，可以在不重新训练模型的情况下显著减少计算开销，为受限环境下的高效推理提供了有效的后训练优化方案。

Abstract: Binary neural networks are very attractive for constrained deployment, enabling small footprint and low-power inference. For binary activations, the dot products become sign-controlled additions or subtractions, but the number of operations is unchanged. Indeed, every neuron or output channel still accumulates all of its input, even though only the sign will be retained, which is often wasteful. As the accumulation progresses, the running partial sum frequently drifts so far from zero that its final sign becomes highly predictable long before the last term is reached; every contribution evaluated after that point changes the value of the sum but not the final output activation. This paper turns this observation into a post-training early-stopping mechanism. We characterize the behavior of the running accumulations on the training dataset and use this information to predict the final sign as soon as possible. No model parameter is retrained. We count the number of operations under an idealized ordering of weights. On VGG11 applied to the CIFAR-10 dataset, the method removes $86.6\%$ of the accumulation terms of the deepest convolution for a $0.37$-point accuracy drop, and $25\%$ of the full-network arithmetic when used on the three deepest convolutions simultaneously, for a $1.36$-point drop.

</details>


### [175] [SAGA: Score-Weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment for Low-Resource Nordic Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06179)
*Hoda Fakharzadehjahromy,Emil Wiman,Andreas Bueff,Hafsteinn Einarsson,Fredrik Heintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGA框架利用依存句法分析器替代人工标注，成功在低资源语言上实现偏好优化，显著提升语法质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的偏好优化方法依赖昂贵的人工偏好标注，在形态丰富的低资源语言中难以扩展，因为这些语言的标注数据稀缺。

Method: 提出SAGA（Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment）框架，利用依存句法分析器监督替代人工标注：1)将分析器判断转换为偏好对用于delta-DPO；2)结合分析器质量和词汇多样性构建复合奖励；3)使用奖励差距标准过滤低信息对；4)监控奖励黑客以保持可靠监督。

Result: 在丹麦语、冰岛语和挪威语（Bokmål）上使用GPT-SW3-1.3B进行实验：丹麦语解析成功率从69.0%提升至93.8%；冰岛语在独立Stanza评估上提升+4.5个百分点，母语者在80%的成对比较中偏好SAGA输出；挪威语Bokmål提升+28个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于解析器的监督是人工偏好标注的实用替代方案，可在具备高质量依存分析器的低资源语言中有效进行语法对齐，无需人工偏好标签。

Abstract: Preference optimisation has proven effective for improving large language models but typically relies on costly human preference annotations. Extending these methods to morphologically rich, low-resource languages remains challenging because such annotations are scarce. We present SAGA (Score-weighted Adaptive Generation Alignment), a parser-guided preference optimisation framework that replaces human labels with dependency-parser supervision. SAGA converts parser judgements into preference pairs for delta-DPO, combines parser quality with lexical diversity in a composite reward, filters low-information pairs using a reward-gap criterion, and monitors reward hacking to maintain reliable supervision. Across Danish, Icelandic, and Norwegian Bokmål using GPT-SW3-1.3B, SAGA consistently improves grammatical quality without requiring human preference labels. Danish parse success increases from 69.0% to 93.8%, Icelandic achieves a +4.5 percentage-point improvement on an independent Stanza evaluation (three-run mean +3.3 percentage points) while native speakers prefer SAGA outputs in 80% of pairwise comparisons, and Norwegian Bokmål improves by +28 percentage points. These results demonstrate that parser-derived supervision is a practical alternative to human preference annotation for grammatical alignment in low-resource languages where high-quality dependency parsers are available.

</details>


### [176] [Continual Learning in Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06216)
*Zhiyan Hou,Dan Zhang,Tao Feng,Liyuan Wang,Wei Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Hongyan An,Junfeng Fang,Haokai Ma,Zhaohui Xu,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持续学习正从参数中心化向系统级适应转变，通过“何时、如何、何地”三维框架系统综述了该领域的范式演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习主要关注参数中心的更新与保持机制，但新兴范式（如策略内学习、测试时训练、外部组件等）已将其边界拓展至系统级适应。为系统刻画这一范式转变，需建立新的分析框架以全面审视持续学习的发展脉络。

Method: 提出一个“何时、如何、何地”三维分析框架：“何时”维度覆盖预训练、后训练及推理阶段；“如何”维度涵盖策略外、策略内及超越梯度的优化机制；“何地”维度区分内部参数更新与外部结构约束。基于此框架系统综述代表性方法并追踪范式演进。

Result: 系统梳理了持续学习从参数中心化向系统级适应转变的技术路径，清晰展示了在时间维度、机制维度与空间维度上的演进脉络与代表性工作。

Conclusion: 持续学习正处于从参数中心化学习向系统级适应的关键转型期，该范式转变带来了新的挑战与机遇，未来需在系统层面探索更广泛的能力边界与适应机制。

Abstract: Classical continual learning (CL) has primarily focused on enabling models to update and retain knowledge through parameter-centric mechanisms, e.g., training strategies, architectural designs, and weight adaptation. However, emerging paradigms are reshaping the scope of CL beyond this traditional model adaptation view. For instance, on-policy learning broadens the space of update mechanisms; test-time training extends CL from the training phase to inference; and external harness components such as memory, skill libraries, and interaction protocols extend the evolutionary boundaries of model capabilities far beyond the static parameter space. Collectively, these developments indicate a transition from parameter-centric learning toward system-level adaptation. To characterize this transition, we examine the evolution of continual learning through three dimensions: When, How, and Where learning occurs. The How dimension encompasses off-policy, on-policy, and beyond-gradient optimization mechanics. The When dimension captures evolution across pre-training, post-training, and inference-time stages. The Where dimension delineates updates occurring within internal parameters versus external structural constraints. Anchored by this tri-axial framework, we systematically survey representative methods, trace the ongoing transition of continual learning, and discuss the key challenges, broader implications, and future directions arising from this paradigm shift.

</details>


### [177] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了后训练适应技术（如微调、检索增强、对齐等），提出了六维分类法，统一了术语定义并揭示了技术演进关系，为模型变更追踪和治理提供了标准化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练适应技术（包括重训练、微调、参数高效适应、对齐、检索增强、模型编辑、遗忘、校准和多模态指令微调等）在现代机器学习中广泛应用。然而，现有文献在不同技术族、模型类别和部署语境下呈现碎片化特征，导致方法间难以比较，也无法清晰描述训练后的模型经历了哪些修改。缺乏统一的分类体系和术语标准，阻碍了技术文档撰写、模型变更追踪和治理分析。

Method: 提出了六维分类法，维度包括：机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围和模型类型。该分类法明确区分了常被混淆的术语（如微调、检索增强和提示），并展示了适应策略从传统机器学习到深度学习、基础模型、大语言模型及多模态大语言模型的演进过程。同时，该方法还刻画了技术间的关系（包括继承、替代、混合和分层部署堆栈），构建了统一的技术词汇表。

Result: 建立了统一的后训练适应技术分类体系和术语表，清晰区分了易混淆概念，揭示了技术演进路径和相互关系。该分类法可应用于技术文档撰写、模型变更追踪和治理分析，为跨技术族的方法比较提供了标准化框架。

Conclusion: 识别了后训练适应领域的关键开放性挑战，包括：评估方法、可复现性、持久性推理时适应、遗忘技术、多模态适应以及治理感知的后训练工作流程。这些挑战为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [178] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了代谢组学专用大语言模型MetaboLLM及其图预测框架MetaboLLM-GIN，通过持续预训练、监督微调与结构化检索构建领域专用模型，将生化描述转换为可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，在多项任务与临床应用中显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散于异构资源中，难以转化为可预测的结构化表示，导致领域知识的整合与应用受限。

Method: 提出MetaboLLM：代谢组学专用大语言模型，采用持续预训练、监督微调与结构化检索进行领域适配；同时提出MetaboLLM-GIN：将生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图，利用图同构网络进行患者级别预测。

Result: 在四种骨干模型家族上，MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系与描述任务中均优于对应基础与医学适配模型，并成功迁移至外部公开基准；MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉旁路移植术后应激性高血糖预测（AUC=0.8616）及绝经后激素方案分类（AUC=0.8123）中取得最高AUC，超越传统模型、其他图构建方法及未适配或非检索配置生成的图；模型解释在两项应用中均产生具有生物学意义的发现。

Conclusion: 领域专用语言模型能够将异构生化知识组织为可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，为代谢组学知识的整合与临床预测提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [179] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了基于缩合反应图（CRG）的自监督对比学习框架RxnCLF，用于反应表示学习。在多个反应产率预测基准测试中，RxnCLF显著优于图和序列基线方法，并在R²指标上取得最佳性能，为反应信息学任务提供了可扩展的基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应表示方法（基于字符串、指纹和图）在复杂底物反应中表现不佳，主要受限于标记数据稀缺、反应空间组合爆炸且稀疏分布。现有方法只能部分捕捉化学转化信息，导致对复杂底物反应的预测准确性不足。

Method: 提出RxnCLF框架，核心是构建缩合反应图（CRG），将反应物和产物信息统一到单一图中，使模型能够学习显式且丰富的转化结构。采用自监督对比学习方法，在170万条Pistachio反应数据上进行预训练，学习紧凑连续的潜在空间，同时捕捉反应中心特征和侧链上下文信息。

Result: 在多个产率预测基准测试（包括Buchwald-Hartwig、钯催化BH偶联、专有HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成数据集）上，RxnCLF在R²指标上显著优于图和序列基线方法，取得整体最佳性能。模型展现出变换感知能力和化学可解释性。

Conclusion: 基于CRG的RxnCLF展示了作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力，能够跨更广泛反应空间泛化，并支持多种下游反应信息学任务，包括区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [180] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了自适应与非自适应模型评估之间的差距，证明了可学习性的充分必要条件，并发现自适应测试最多可将查询复杂度降低二次方因子（Θ(N²)），但无法达到指数级的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型评估中，一个核心问题是在观测响应之前固定所有测试（非自适应），还是根据早期响应选择后续测试（自适应）。作者旨在量化这两种策略在条件查询模型中的效率差异，揭示交互式评估究竟能带来多大的查询复杂度优势。

Method: 作者在有限结果空间𝒳（|𝒳|=N）上建立条件查询模型，研究分布类的可分性条件，并构造非自适应随机化过程来模拟自适应策略的转录本，同时设计匹配的分布族以建立下界。

Result: （1）可学习性成立的充要条件是两类分布在成对条件概率上存在正分离；（2）若分离为零，最优最坏情况误差精确为1/2；（3）对于任意T查询自适应策略，存在使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))次成对查询的非自适应过程，其模拟转录本与自适应转录本在总变差距离内不超过ρ；（4）构造了自适应查询复杂度为常数但非自适应复杂度为Ω(N²)的分布族。

Conclusion: 最坏情况下固定误差的自适应与非自适应查询复杂度之间的差距为Θ(N²)，表明交互式评估可将测试次数降低二次方因子，但其外观上的指数分支并未转化为指数级的查询优势。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [181] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SG-TULA算法，用于从具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸势能的目标分布中采样，并在Wasserstein-2距离下获得了改进的非渐近收敛界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究从势能同时具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性质的目标分布中采样的问题。现有基于次梯度的朗之万算法在收敛率方面存在不足，且缺乏可比较的非渐近保证。

Method: 引入次梯度驯服未调整朗之万算法（SG-TULA），这是朗之万扩散的离散化方法，直接在次梯度上操作，无需计算昂贵的平滑过程。采用驯服技术来处理超线性情况，产生稳定的显式方案。

Result: 在Wasserstein-2距离下推导出非渐近收敛界，所有常数都以维度和逆温度的形式明确追踪，改进了当前已知的基于次梯度的朗之万算法的收敛率。同时为相关优化问题提供了过量风险估计。

Conclusion: 在GPT-2系列的LLM正则化预训练势能上验证了假设（具有显式常数），SG-TULA的增强坐标变体在预训练方面与经过微调的AdamW和Muon竞争性表现良好，而后者目前没有可比较的非渐近保证。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [182] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Surv-IPTB模型，通过注意力机制和配对患者比较来估计个体治疗获益概率(IPTB)，在复杂非线性场景中显著优于传统元学习器基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中估计个体治疗获益概率(IPTB)是一个重要但具有挑战性的问题。现有方法在处理右删失数据和复杂非线性结构时性能受限，缺乏对治疗效应不确定性的合理量化。需要开发一种能够准确估计IPTB、妥善处理删失数据、并在非线性场景中保持稳健性能的方法。

Method: 1) 将IPTB估计重构为二分类问题，通过治疗组和对照组间的配对患者比较实现；2) 采用不精确概率表示来处理右删失观测，用区间值概率刻画不确定的治疗效应；3) 引入具有可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，实现配对比较的灵活数据驱动聚合，同时学习删失案例的软类别概率；4) 构建端到端的注意力框架进行IPTB估计。

Result: 在具有复杂非线性结构（螺旋、钟形、圆形特征空间）的合成数据集上进行广泛实验，结果表明：1) 模型在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下保持稳健性能；2) 显著优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的元学习器基线（T-learner和S-learner）；3) 在传统方法性能严重退化的挑战性非线性场景中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于注意力的框架为生存分析场景下的个性化治疗获益评估提供了一个可扩展且统计原理完备的解决方案，在复杂非线性环境中展现出优越性能，为精准医学决策提供了有效工具。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [183] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BaKron算法，一种高效的神经网络量化求解器，利用双面Kronecker分解Hessian近似加速量化过程，计算复杂度从O(m²n²)降低到O(mn(m+n))，与GPTQ的立方级复杂度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPTQ风格自适应舍入算法仅使用来自输入激活的单面信息，而双面Kronecker分解Hessian近似可以捕获输出坐标间的相关性，但直接在向量化权重域应用GPTQ计算成本高昂。需要一种高效的方法来利用更丰富的曲率信息进行量化。

Method: 基于BoA和YAQA使用的双面自适应舍入公式，引入BaKron求解器，结合反对角并行性和递归分治构造。该方法对于m×n权重矩阵使用O(m+n)顺序步骤，将总工作量从O(m²n²)减少到O(mn(m+n))，在量化器和Hessian估计器方面均具有模块化特性。

Result: BaKron匹配了GPTQ的立方级缩放特性，同时利用了更丰富的曲率信息。算法在保持计算效率的同时能够更好地捕获权重矩阵的几何特性，提供了实用的基准测试和实验评估。

Conclusion: BaKron成功地在保持与GPTQ相当的计算复杂度的同时，利用了双面Hessian信息提供的更丰富的曲率信息，为神经网络量化提供了一种更高效的解决方案，具有良好的模块化和实用性。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [184] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出U-OPSD方法，实现了无需外部监督的纯自蒸馏，通过内部一致性和多数投票构建伪解，在多个数学基准测试上性能提升8.5%-10.7%，达到或超越有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在策略自蒸馏方法（OPD/OPSD）仍严重依赖外部监督信号（真实标签、环境反馈或大模型指导），未能实现真正的"自"蒸馏。研究动机在于探索仅利用模型自身生成内容，通过内部一致性实现完全无监督的在策略自蒸馏。

Method: U-OPSD首先采样多个rollout，在自一致性阈值下通过多数投票构建伪解；然后将教师分布条件化为最短伪解，并将其蒸馏到模型最长错误完成的前缀中，使模型能够在其置信错误的精确位置进行自我纠正。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25、MATH500和AMC23等基准上，Qwen3非思考模式下4B和8B规模分别提升8.5%和10.7%，平均超越OPSD 3.2%和2.3%；思考模式下4B规模超越OPSD 0.9%、8B规模持平，并超越GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: U-OPSD证明了仅通过模型自身生成和内部一致性即可实现有效的自蒸馏，在无需任何外部监督的情况下，性能能够匹敌甚至超越有监督方法（OPSD和GRPO），为LLM后训练提供了一种完全自主的学习范式。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [185] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RRC方法，通过相对偏好排名解决生成式奖励模型在强化学习中的匹配问题，显著提升了RL训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型虽然在响应排序方面表现出色，但在强化学习中未能充分发挥潜力。分析发现，这源于生成式奖励模型的比较性质与现有RL算法采用的标量评分范式之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出基于排名的奖励构建（RRC）方法，包含两种互补策略：自我竞争排名（利用采样响应之间的比较）和锚点引导排名（通过少量参考响应实现可扩展的排名式奖励构建），从而从相对偏好排名中导出奖励信号。

Result: 在开放式聊天和推理基准测试中，RRC显著改善了生成式奖励模型的强化学习训练，相比现有奖励构建方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RRC成功弥合了生成式奖励模型与强化学习算法之间的差距，使生成式奖励模型能够为RL提供更有效的学习信号，实现了范式转换后的技术突破。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [186] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CalibForge，一个自主终端任务合成系统，通过对抗性求解器校准来生成5,431个校准后的终端任务。在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%/47.57%，相比基础模型最高提升30.04个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的终端智能体训练任务验证仅关注可执行性和可解性，但无法确定任务对于特定求解器的难度是否适中。需要一种方法来生成既可解又具有适当挑战性的任务，以提供有效的监督信号。

Method: 提出CalibForge系统，采用两种对抗性求解器校准策略：多求解器校准针对异构求解器池中的分歧，对比求解器校准针对强通过/弱失败的指定关系。两种策略都以已验证的可解性为基础，目标是求解器相对的可学习区域。通过求解器行为反馈迭代修订候选任务。

Result: 构建了5,431个校准后的终端任务。消融实验表明两种校准策略比传统人工编写、验证或单一求解器反馈更有效。完整集合训练的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%。相比基础模型，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上提升24.71个百分点，SWE-bench Pro上提升27.68个百分点，Doc2Repo上提升30.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对的可学习性是构建有效且可迁移的智能体训练数据的实用目标，为终端智能体训练数据的质量提升提供了新方向。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [187] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个学习器，在有限VC维假设类中达到了统计最优的风险界限，从而解决了不可知PAC学习的样本复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不可知PAC学习框架下，有限VC维假设类的最优风险界限一直是一个重要但未完全解决的问题。本文的动机是填补这一理论空白，构建一个学习器使其风险上界匹配已知的下界，从而完全确定不可知PAC学习的样本复杂度（在通用常数因子范围内）。

Method: 作者针对VC维为d的假设类H⊆{-1,+1}^X，从n个i.i.d.样本出发，构造了一个学习器hat_h。核心技术贡献是证明了该学习器能够达到风险界限：L(hat_h) ≤ L* + O(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)，其中L*是最小可达到风险，δ是置信参数。该界限具有速率O(√(L*d/n)+d/n)，关键在于对L*的依赖关系。

Result: 主要结果：对于任意固定L*值，本文构造的学习器达到了统计最优风险界限，上界为L(hat_h) ≤ L* + 7·10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n)。这个上界在常数因子意义下匹配Devroye, Györfi和Lugosi（1996）给出的下界，从而在理论上完全解决了不可知PAC学习的样本复杂度问题。

Conclusion: 本文证明了有限VC维假设类的不可知PAC学习具有最优的样本复杂度。所构造的学习器在所有L*>0的情况下都能达到匹配下界的风险界限，这意味着从统计学习理论角度看，不可知PAC学习的最优风险界限已被完全确定。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Hierarchical Server Architecture for Agentic Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05332)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层的动态资源发现架构和软件系统，通过秘书代理实现跨云、边缘和HPC系统的并发异步资源协商、选择和调度，在真实和模拟环境中实现了87.71%的高协商准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体科学（Agentic science）在计算工作、科学流水线和工作负载管理中的兴起，跨机构的科学工作负载需要专门的硬件资源。调度过程需要评估工作负载需求与环境资源的匹配，而自动化的资源发现是实现这一目标的关键前提步骤。

Method: 提出了一种分层的动态架构，设计并实现了秘书代理（secretary agents）来并发异步地处理资源请求的协商、选择和调度。该系统探测并发现了跨越7个类别的51个真实和模拟资源提供者，涵盖了多样化的云、边缘和HPC系统。

Result: 通过19,973次协商模拟和6,952次选择模拟验证了系统的可靠性，实现了87.71%的高协商准确率，且选择成本与传统策略相当。目前系统已支持Genesis Mission任务，证明了架构的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在智能体科学中，代理、发现工具和基础设施之间精心协调的重要性。所提出的架构成功实现了跨异构计算环境的自动化资源发现与调度，为智能体科学工作流提供了可靠的基础设施支撑。

Abstract: Agentic science is transforming the landscape of computational work, extending to scientific pipelines and workload managers. The workloads require specialized hardware within and across institutions. If assessing workload needs against environments is required for scheduling, automated discovery of resources is an essential step. In this paper, we present a hierarchical, dynamic architecture and software to discover resources across diverse cloud, edge, and HPC systems. The design enables concurrent, asynchronous negotiation, selection, and dispatch of requests for work using secretary agents. The agents probe and discover 51 real and simulated providers across 7 categories. We perform 19,973 negotiation and 6,952 selection simulations to assess reliability of decisions, demonstrating high (87.71\%) negotiation accuracy and selection costs comparable to more traditional strategies. Designed for extensibility and currently supporting the Genesis Mission, this architecture exemplifies the importance of careful coordination between agents, discovery tools, and infrastructure for agentic science.

</details>


### [189] [Application Failures and Machine Computational Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05408)
*Carlo Graziani,Bethany Lusch,O. E. Bronson Messer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于使用量（如节点小时）而非时间的新框架，用于评估E级科学计算系统在应用故障率显著情况下的运行效率，并通过Frontier超算数据进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前E级科学计算平台和大型AI训练系统面临显著的故障率问题，而科学计算平台的应用异质性使得传统的基于时间的故障率描述不再适用，需要建立更合理的评估框架。

Method: 提出用使用量（如节点小时）代替传统的时间来描述故障率和平均故障间隔；考虑故障、检查点和重启成本导致的使用量损失；更新Daly (2006)的框架，推导最优检查点间隔以最小化损失，并计算系统计算效率。

Result: 成功推导出系统计算效率公式，该效率表示可用于科学计算的预期资源分配比例，并使用Oak Ridge国家实验室的Frontier超算一年的生产运行数据进行了方法验证。

Conclusion: 对于具有异构应用和显著故障率的E级科学计算系统，基于使用量的故障率描述方法更适合，所提出的框架能为用户确定最优检查点间隔提供指导，从而最大化系统计算效率。

Abstract: We present a framework for evaluating uptime efficiency of Exascale-class scientific computers when application failure rates are appreciable. This is the situation that confronts current leadership-class scientific computing platforms and large AI training installations. What distinguishes scientific computing platforms is the heterogeneity of their applications. We argue that this diversity requires that failure rates and mean intervals between failures should be specified in terms of \emph{usage} (e.g. node-hours) rather than time, as is currently customary. We consider the usage loss terms due to failures, to checkpointing, and to restart costs, and update the framework of Daly (2006) allowing users to specify optimal checkpointing usage intervals that minimize such losses. We derive the machine computational efficiency, which specifies the expected fractional resource allocation that is available for scientific computation. We illustrate the methodology using one year of production runtime data from the \emph{Frontier} supercomputer at Oak Ridge National Laboratory.

</details>


### [190] [Serverless platform driven CPU loadbalancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05633)
*Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个由Serverless平台驱动的CPU负载均衡框架，通过基于SchedExt的自定义调度器将CPU调度决策暴露给控制平面，实现了约15%的能耗降低和高达50%的请求延迟减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Serverless平台虽然维护着函数调用和资源利用的全局视图，但CPU调度决策主要依赖于操作系统调度器，平台无法直接利用其全局信息来优化调度。这种分离限制了平台在能耗和性能方面的优化潜力。

Method: 提出了一个基于SchedExt(SCX)的自定义Linux调度器，引入了可配置的调度域和共享接口。控制平面可以根据函数的历史到达间隔时间将函数分配到不同的调度域。在每个域内，采用单队列负载均衡策略结合虚拟时间优先级策略来改善任务放置，同时减少忙轮询任务的干扰。通过八域配置实现了最佳的性能与能耗权衡。

Result: 八域配置实现了最佳权衡：系统能耗降低约15%，调用成本仅增加5%。在重负载工作负载下，与默认Linux CFS调度器相比，请求延迟减少高达50%。证明了将CPU调度决策暴露给Serverless控制平面可以同时改善能耗效率和负载性能。

Conclusion: 将CPU调度决策暴露给Serverless控制平面是可行且有益的，可以在保持调度灵活性的同时显著改善能耗效率和负载性能。这种方法为Serverless平台的资源管理提供了新的优化维度。

Abstract: Serverless platforms maintain a global view of function invocations and resource utilization, yet existing systems largely restrict CPU scheduling decisions to the operating system scheduler. This paper presents a serverless platform-driven CPU load balancing framework that enables the control plane to directly influence CPU scheduling through a custom Linux scheduler built on SchedExt(SCX). The proposed scheduler introduces configurable scheduling domains and a shared interface that allows the control plane to assign functions to domains based on their historical inter-arrival times. Within each domain, a single-queue load-balancing strategy combined with a virtual-time prioritization policy improves task placement while reducing interference from busy-polling tasks. Results show that an eight-domain configuration achieves the best trade-off, reducing system energy consumption by approximately 15% while increasing invocation cost by only 5%. Under heavily loaded workloads, the proposed scheduler also reduces request latency by up to 50% compared to the default Linux Completely Fair Scheduler (CFS). These results demonstrate that exposing CPU scheduling decisions to the serverless control plane can improve both energy efficiency and workload performance while preserving scheduling flexibility.

</details>


### [191] [RepoOMP: Repository-Aware Hotspot OpenMP Parallelization via Dependency-Aware Context Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05855)
*Yongjie Qian,Ke Gao,Zhibin Zhang,Shaohui Peng,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了RepoOMP框架，通过混合规则与LLM代理的方法，实现了对成熟仓库中热点代码的OpenMP并行化，平均加速比达5-9倍，并显著降低了token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成熟仓库中热点代码的OpenMP并行化面临两大挑战：基于规则的工具在合法性无法局部证明时会欠并行化，而基于代理的方法在检索遗漏关键依赖或包含无关代码时会变得不稳定。需要一种能够有效收集并行化相关证据的方法。

Method: 提出RepoOMP混合框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)构建多粒度属性性能图(MAP)；(2)在确定性规则和LLM代理之间路由热点；(3)构建结构化转换上下文(STC)，暴露依赖事实而不向模型提供无关代码，实现证据引导的并行化工作流。

Result: 在来自NPB、BOTS、FFmpeg、NCNN和GROMACS的951个热点上评估，372个热点被接受（包括330个真实仓库热点）。NPB平均加速8.23倍，BOTS平均加速8.96倍。9个真实世界内核交叉骨干平均加速5.25倍，加速比提升18-28%，token成本降低47-68%。330个真实世界热点中位数加速2.25倍。

Conclusion: RepoOMP为仓库环境下的热点并行化提供了有效的证据引导工作流，在保持合法性的同时实现了显著的性能提升和成本节约，为成熟仓库的并行化提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: OpenMP parallelization of hotspots in mature repositories remains difficult because loop safety and optimization payoff often depend on non-local evidence. Rule-based tools under-parallelize when legality is not locally provable, while agent-based approaches become unstable when retrieval misses decisive dependencies or includes irrelevant code. We present RepoOMP, a hybrid framework that recovers parallelization-relevant evidence before generation. RepoOMP builds a Multi-granularity Attributes Performance graph (MAP), routes hotspots between deterministic rules and an LLM agent, and constructs a Structured Transformation Context (STC) that exposes dependency facts without flooding the model with unrelated repository text. We evaluate RepoOMP on 951 profiled hotspots from NPB, BOTS, FFmpeg, NCNN, and GROMACS. Under compilation, workload-specific checks, and positive speedup, 372 hotspots are accepted, including 330 real-world repository hotspots. RepoOMP achieves average speedups of $8.23\times$ on NPB and $8.96\times$ on BOTS. For the nine detailed real-world kernels used in matched-backbone and robustness analyses, RepoOMP reaches a cross-backbone mean of $5.25\times$, improves speedup by 18--28\%, and reduces agent-side token cost by 47--68\% relative to the unstructured Claude Code baseline. Across 330 accepted real-world hotspots, median speedup is $2.25\times$. Overall, RepoOMP provides an evidence-guided workflow for hotspot parallelization in repository settings. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Qlalq/RepoOMP_Simplified.

</details>


### [192] [Operating Multi-Node Full Fine-Tuning on NVIDIA B300: A Field Report on Telemetry-Based Triage, Negative Results, and Operational Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05944)
*Seon Ho Kim,Ui Jeong Jeon,Su Hyeon Kim,Min Tae Hwang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文报告了在16张NVIDIA B300 GPU上全量微调32.76B参数密集模型（Qwen3-32B）的实践经验，提供了功率校准表、负面实验结果、强扩展性数据及失败案例分析等四项实用成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补NVIDIA B300加速器上大规模模型训练的实地经验空白，提供校准测量数据和实用指南，帮助从业者避免常见优化误区和识别隐蔽故障。

Method: 采用FSDP/ZeRO-3分布式策略在双节点16张B300 GPU上全量微调Qwen3-32B模型，通过对照实验、功率监测、强扩展性测试和失败案例分析，系统性地评估硬件性能和训练稳定性。

Result: 提供了（1）B300功率诊断表区分计算/通信/数据饥饿/检查点/空闲状态；（2）NFS读取与本地缓存性能相当（约53k tok/s），揭示吞吐崩溃源于NFS/CPU竞争；（3）近线性强扩展性数据；（4）识别并解决epoch-end NCCL死锁，通过预运行不变量门控将数小时静默失败转为即时拒绝。

Conclusion: 对于数据相关的数据并行作业，应监控功率而非利用率，并在启动前验证不变量——通过的冒烟测试并不保证完整运行的安全性。

Abstract: We report operational experience full-fine-tuning a 32.76B-parameter dense model (Qwen3-32B) on 16 x NVIDIA B300 (two nodes, FSDP / ZeRO-3) -- among the first published field accounts on this accelerator. We claim no new algorithm. The individual mechanisms we use are established practice; our contribution is the integrated field experience and a set of calibrated measurements on new hardware. Concretely we offer four practitioner artifacts. (1) A B300-calibrated power-draw triage table that distinguishes compute / communication / data-starvation / checkpoint-or-deadlock / idle by board wattage (utilization% reads 100% during an NCCL hang). (2) A set of honest negative results that dispel common optimization folklore at this scale: a controlled A/B in which per-step NFS reading matches a pretokenized local cache (~53k tok/s) because the corpus fits in page cache and the job is compute-bound; and a reconstruction of an earlier "throughput collapse" as NFS/CPU contention rather than a storage-medium limit. (3) Calibrated 4/8/16-GPU strong-scaling and GPU-hour numbers on B300 (near-linear, as expected in this regime; we report absolute values as reference data). (4) A worked failure case -- an epoch-end NCCL deadlock from per-rank token-packing imbalance -- together with a 2.7-second pre-run invariant gate and an external watcher that turn multi-hour silent failures into instant rejections. This deadlock and its remedy correspond to PyTorch's documented Join / equalize-to-minimum practice; we position our instantiation against that prior art and report the GPU-hours the failure cost and the gate saves. The transferable takeaway is operational, not algorithmic: for data-dependent data-parallel jobs, watch power rather than utilization, and verify invariants before launch -- a passing smoke test is not evidence of a safe full run.

</details>


### [193] [TensorCast: The Missing Tensor Management Layer in Large Language Model Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06007)
*Yuhan Zhou,Yuchu Luo,Hao Nie,Wangrunze Lv,Yu Zhou,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Chenren Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TensorCast系统，通过引入TaaS抽象层解耦张量状态管理与计算逻辑，在保持与专用系统相当性能的同时，实现了跨组件优化策略，在多轮代理工作负载中将TTFT中位数降低高达93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基础设施针对模型权重加载、KV缓存管理、检查点同步等单个张量管理任务进行了深度优化，但这种特化创建了孤立的管理孤岛，阻碍了张量管理策略在演进LLM工作负载中的重用与组合，缺乏统一的张量生命周期管理抽象层。

Method: 提出Tensor-as-a-Service (TaaS)抽象，设计与实现TensorCast分布式张量管理层，提供一等公民张量抽象、可编程生命周期原语，以及将张量管理策略与执行机制分离的运行时，使开发者能够通过API编写张量管理程序并透明利用分布式执行与数据移动能力。

Result: TensorCast与vLLM和SGLang集成，在模型权重实例化、权重同步、KV缓存管理、可编程请求路由等多种张量生命周期工作负载上实现了与专用张量管理系统相当的性能；通过可编程策略在高并发多轮代理工作负载下将TTFT中位数改善高达93.2%。

Conclusion: 张量生命周期管理是LLM基础设施中缺失的关键抽象层，TensorCast通过解耦张量状态管理与计算逻辑，既保持了竞争力性能，又使新的跨组件优化策略成为可能，为演进中的LLM工作负载提供了统一、可编程的张量管理解决方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM infrastructure increasingly manages tensors not only as computation data, but also as persistent states shared across distributed components. Existing systems optimize individual tensor management tasks, such as model weight loading, KV cache management, and checkpoint synchronization, by deeply integrating task-specific mechanisms with execution engines, networks, or storage backends. However, this specialization creates isolated silos that hinder the reuse and composition of tensor management strategies across evolving LLM workloads. In this paper, we identify tensor lifecycle management as a missing abstraction layer in LLM infrastructure and propose Tensor-as-a-Service (TaaS), which decouples tensor state management from computation logic. We design and build TensorCast, a distributed tensor management layer that provides first-class tensor abstractions, programmable lifecycle primitives, and a runtime that separates tensor management policies from execution mechanisms. This enables developers to write tensor management programs using TensorCast APIs while transparently leveraging distributed execution and data movement. We integrate TensorCast with vLLM and SGLang and evaluate it across diverse tensor lifecycle workloads, including model weight materialization, weight synchronization, KV cache management, and programmable request routing. Our results show that TensorCast achieves competitive performance with specialized tensor management systems while enabling new cross-component optimization policies. A programmable policy implemented with TensorCast improves median TTFT by up to 93.2% under highly concurrent multi-turn agent workloads.

</details>


### [194] [Routing LLM Inference to the Cleanest Grid in Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06188)
*Aleks Bernhard,Arif Baran Yardimci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究验证了基于边际运行排放率(MOER)信号的碳感知推理路由的可行性，在多区域GPU测试平台上实现了50.9%的GPU运行排放减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理是快速增长的电力负载，其边际碳强度在不同电网区域和一天中的变化超过一个数量级，使请求放置成为一个有吸引力的杠杆——无需重新训练或硬件更改。

Method: 在多区域GPU测试床上进行碳感知推理路由的实时验证，使用边际运行排放率(MOER)信号驱动；采用生产压力路由器作为盲基线（而非统一放置）；通过测量的并发曲线从GPU遥测(NVIDIA DCGM)归属每个请求的能耗，而非铭牌TDP；对每个请求进行历史MOER碳结算，而不仅是驱动决策的预测值。

Result: 核心实时结果是可行性：MOER信号成功引导跨区域推理，无调度失败；回放一年小时级MOER数据，在主要配置下碳感知放置相比轮询减少50.9%的GPU可归属运行排放(95%置信区间48.5-53.3%)；小时级最低MOER路由贡献约22.4个百分点，约占放置减少的40%。

Conclusion: 碳感知推理路由在实践中可行且有效；跨区域比较时应按绝对MOER排序而非区域内标准化的百分位信号索引；这是针对特定车队和历史年份的建模结果，非普适估计。

Abstract: Large-language-model inference is a fast-growing electricity load whose marginal carbon intensity varies by more than an order of magnitude across grid regions and across the day, making request placement an attractive lever: no retraining, no hardware change. We report a live validation of carbon-aware inference routing on multi-region GPU testbeds driven by marginal operating emissions rate (MOER) signals, with three properties uncommon in prior work: a blind baseline that is an actual production pressure-based router rather than uniform placement; per-request energy attributed from GPU telemetry (NVIDIA DCGM) via measured concurrency curves rather than nameplate TDP; and carbon settlement of every request against historical MOER, not only the forecast that drove the decision. The central live result is feasibility: a MOER signal steered inference across regions with no observed dispatch failures, as a strict and reversible overlay on the production router. To size the effect, we replay a year of hourly MOER across a grid-diverse CONUS fleet. In the primary modeled configuration, carbon-aware placement reduces modeled GPU-attributable operational emissions by 50.9% versus round-robin (95% block-bootstrap CI 48.5-53.3%). Because the replay dispatches against historical MOER rather than a forecast, this is an upper bound under that configuration; forecast error would reduce operationally realized savings. Before session pinning, hourly lowest-MOER routing contributes about 22.4 percentage points, roughly 40% of the 54.0% placement reduction, beyond a static annual-mean-MOER policy. These are modeled results for one fleet and historical year, not a universal estimate. We also record a practical observation: when comparing regions, rank by absolute MOER rather than the percentile signal-index, which is normalized within each region and answers a temporal, not a spatial, question.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Multi-Agent Transformer for Queue-Level XR Traffic Scheduling in TSN Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05340)
*Marcos Carvalho,Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Daniel F. Macedo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体Transformer的TSN队列级调度方法，用于解决MEC环境中XR流量的动态调度问题，实现了显著的延迟和失败率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSN和MEC在支持XR等时敏应用方面具有巨大潜力，但XR在MEC环境中的广泛部署面临严峻挑战：(1)共存服务导致共享网络资源竞争；(2)XR流量类型具有不同特性和时序关键性要求；(3)现有强化学习方法采用集中式或高层多智能体设计，主要针对周期性工业流量，难以捕获队列间依赖关系，且对高度动态异构的XR流量适应性差。

Method: 提出了一种队列级XR流量调度方法：(1)采用多智能体强化学习框架，实现队列级精细控制；(2)引入多智能体Transformer(MAT)架构，通过智能体观测和动作的注意力机制来建模队列间依赖关系；(3)实现异构共存XR应用间的隐式协调。

Result: 仿真实验表明：(1)延迟降低最高达71.42%；(2)失败率降低最高达83.2%；(3)所有队列均能持续实现高可靠性；(4)性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于MAT的多智能体强化学习方法有效解决了XR流量调度中的队列间依赖建模和动态适应性问题，为MEC环境下的异构XR应用提供了高效的队列级调度解决方案，显著提升了系统性能和可靠性。

Abstract: Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) and Mobile Edge Computing (MEC) hold strong potential for enabling ultra-reliable low-latency communication for time-sensitive applications, such as eXtended Reality (XR). However, the widespread adoption of XR introduces significant challenges due to co-located services in MEC environments, leading to contention for shared network resources. Moreover, XR traffic types have distinct characteristics and criticality in terms of timing requirements, further increasing the complexity and dynamics of such environments. Although reinforcement learning has shown promise for TSN scheduling optimization in dynamic network scenarios, existing approaches rely on centralized or high-level multi-agent designs and are typically tailored to periodic and predictable industrial traffic, limiting their applicability to XR workloads. As a result, these approaches suffer from (i) limited ability to capture inter-queue dependencies due to coarse-grained control, and (ii) poor adaptability to highly dynamic and heterogeneous XR traffic. To address these gaps, we propose a multi-agent reinforcement learning approach for queue-level XR traffic scheduling. We adopt the multi-agent transformer (MAT) to model inter-queue dependencies via attention over agents' observations and actions, enabling implicit coordination across heterogeneous co-located XR applications. Our simulation results show that the proposed method outperforms baselines, achieving up to 71.42% latency reduction and up to 83.2% reduction in failure rate, while consistently achieving high reliability across all queues.

</details>


### [196] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Online Traffic Scheduling in Time-Sensitive Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05346)
*Marcos Carvalho,Fatih Temiz,Shavbo Salehi,Melike Erol-Kantarci,Daniel F. Macedo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于异构近端策略优化（HAPPO）的多智能体强化学习框架，用于TSN调度，在动态XR驱动的MEC场景中，平均帧等待时间降低26.8%，最坏情况延迟降低约16.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSN调度方案主要依赖静态优化技术或基于固定流量模式的集中式学习模型，在动态MEC环境中效果受限。实际MEC环境常承载多个共置的XR流量流，其特征随时间演变，产生复杂的队列间依赖关系，而现有调度器无法有效捕捉这些动态依赖。因此需要能够适应动态流量条件、协调多个TSN队列的去中心化自适应调度机制。

Method: 提出多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架用于TSN调度，将每个TSN队列建模为自主智能体。采用异构智能体近端策略优化（HAPPO）算法显式建模智能体间依赖关系，实现跨队列的联合服务交付优化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在动态XR驱动的MEC场景中，平均帧等待时间降低高达26.8%，最坏情况延迟降低约16.8%，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于HAPPO的MARL框架能够有效解决动态MEC环境中多队列TSN调度问题，通过显式建模队列间依赖关系并实现去中心化协调，显著降低了帧等待时间和延迟，为动态XR应用场景提供了有效的调度解决方案。

Abstract: Time-sensitive networking (TSN) is increasingly integrated into mobile edge computing (MEC) to support applications with stringent latency requirements, such as extended reality (XR). However, existing TSN scheduling solutions predominantly rely on static optimization techniques or centralized learning models that are based on fixed traffic patterns, limiting their effectiveness in dynamic environments. In practice, MEC environments often host multiple co-located XR traffic flows whose characteristics evolve over time, creating complex inter-queue dependencies that current schedulers fail to capture. Addressing these challenges requires adaptive, decentralized scheduling mechanisms capable of coordinating multiple TSN queues under varying traffic conditions. To this end, this paper proposes a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) framework for TSN scheduling, where each TSN queue is modeled as an autonomous agent. The Heterogeneous-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (HAPPO) algorithm is employed to explicitly model inter-agent dependencies and jointly optimize service delivery across queues. The simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces average frame waiting times by up to 26.8% and worst-case delays by approximately 16.8%, highlighting its effectiveness in dynamic XR-driven MEC scenarios.

</details>


### [197] [DTMC-Based Analysis and Scheduling for Periodic Flows with Proactive HARQ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05639)
*Haozhe Yi,Junyi Liu,Maolin Yang,Haochun Liang,Bo Liu,Feng Hong,Chaowei Liu,Hongbiao Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散时间马尔可夫链(DTMC)的框架，用于分析采用 proactive HARQ 的周期性 URLLC 业务的可调度性，该方法显著提高了满足严格可靠性和时延约束的调度成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超可靠低时延通信(URLLC)需要对异构周期性业务提供严格的可靠性和时延保证。现有的 proactive HARQ 虽能通过提前终止提高资源效率，但其时隙级时序效应（尤其是反馈延迟）使得可调度性分析变得复杂。现有方法难以准确建模这些跨时隙的时序影响。

Method: 本文提出了一个基于离散时间马尔可夫链(DTMC)的框架来建模采用 proactive HARQ 的周期性流。通过扩展状态空间，该模型能够精确捕获 HARQ 往返时间(RTT)及其他跨时隙时序效应。框架可确定满足异构可靠性和时延约束所需的传输机会，并通过两阶段遗传算法支持偏移调度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与 reactive HARQ、K-Repetition 方案以及无保证的 proactive HARQ 相比，所提方法在工业 URLLC 业务场景下实现了更高的可调度性，同时计算开销处于可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了 proactive HARQ 场景下周期性 URLLC 业务的可调度性分析难题，为工业 URLLC 系统提供了一种有效的调度分析工具。

Abstract: Ultra-Reliable Low-Latency Communication (URLLC) requires strict reliability and latency guarantees for heterogeneous periodic traffic. Proactive HARQ improves resource efficiency through early termination, but slot-level timing effects, particularly delayed feedback, complicate schedulability analysis.
  This paper presents a discrete-time Markov chain (DTMC)-based framework for periodic flows with proactive HARQ. By expanding the state space, the model captures HARQ round-trip time and other cross-slot timing effects. The framework determines the transmission opportunities required to satisfy heterogeneous reliability and latency constraints and supports offset-based scheduling through a two-stage genetic algorithm.
  Simulations with industrial URLLC traffic show that the proposed method achieves higher schedulability than reactive HARQ, K-Repetition, and non-guaranteed proactive HARQ, with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [198] [Closed-Loop Decision-Focused Learning for User-Aware Cloud Orchestration under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05735)
*Dongbin Jiao,Xubo Zhang,Huakang Lin,Ke Shang,Shi Yan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对云计算资源调度问题，提出CL-DFL闭环决策聚焦学习框架，解决了传统PTO方法的两阶段解耦问题，在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间实现了更优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变云工作负载导致非高峰期资源利用率低和高峰期资源竞争问题，现有预测-优化（PTO）框架存在两阶段解耦问题，难以平衡违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率。

Method: 提出CL-DFL框架，将异构作业调度建模为不确定约束下的多目标组合优化问题（MOCOP）。框架集成了基于MTGNN的时空预测器和基于TPE的零阶决策聚焦学习机制，建立资源感知与调度决策之间的端到端反馈通路。同时开发GNeuro-PLS策略，将群组相对策略优化（GRPO）融入协作局部搜索以提升异构工作负载下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，CL-DFL在违规率、用户满意度和资源利用率之间实现了优越的权衡。在常规工作负载下有效控制过载风险，在高饱和场景下相比最先进基线方法保持了更好的韧性。

Conclusion: CL-DFL框架通过建立端到端反馈通路成功解决了传统PTO方法的解耦问题，为云编排提供了一种有效的闭环决策聚焦学习解决方案，在多种工作负载场景下均表现出色。

Abstract: Time-varying cloud workloads often cause resource under-utilization during off-peak periods and resource contention during peak periods. Existing prediction-then-optimization (PTO) frameworks suffer from two-stage decoupling, hindering the balance among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. We formulate heterogeneous job scheduling as a multi-objective combinatorial optimization problem (MOCOP) under uncertain constraints and propose a closed-loop decision-focused learning (CL-DFL) framework for cloud orchestration. CL-DFL integrates a Multivariate Time-series Graph Neural Network (MTGNN)-based spatio-temporal predictor with a zeroth-order decision-focused learning (DFL) mechanism based on the tree-structured Parzen estimator (TPE). This integration establishes an end-to-end (E2E) feedback pathway between resource perception and scheduling decisions. Furthermore, we develop the GNeuro-PLS strategy by incorporating group relative policy optimization (GRPO) into cooperative local search to improve robustness under heterogeneous workloads. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that CL-DFL achieves superior trade-offs among violation rate, user satisfaction, and resource utilization. It effectively controls overload risks under regular workloads and maintains resilience under highly saturated scenarios compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [199] [5G ISAC-Based UAV Detection and 3-D Tracking Using Uplink Sounding Reference Signals on an End-to-End O-RAN Simulation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05826)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan,Shiva R. Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于开源组件的5G ISAC端到端O-RAN仿真测试平台，利用上行探测参考信号实现被动雷达功能，在无需修改NR标准的前提下实现了无人机检测与三维跟踪，并验证了高度可观测性的两种解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）技术允许蜂窝基础设施在同一波形上同时服务通信用户和执行感知任务。针对低空无人机检测与三维跟踪的应用需求，现有系统面临高度不可观测的挑战，需要验证端到端系统实现的可行性并解决高度估计问题。

Method: 构建基于OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT的端到端O-RAN仿真测试平台，复用NR上行探测参考信号（SRS）作为被动雷达波形。在gNB内部设计PHY层感知阶段产生检测结果，通过自定义E2服务模型传递给扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）跟踪xApp。采用两种独立方法解决高度不可观测问题：平面接收阵列提供垂直孔径，以及第二发射机提供距离分集。同时进行离线射线追踪研究验证。

Result: 两种实时实验方案均验证了离线射线追踪研究的结果，估计器从故意错误的初始高度收敛至1.8米RMSE，在信号处理链和端到端实时协议栈中均确立了高度可观测性。在并发10 Mbps上行通信负载下，检测覆盖范围得以保持。

Conclusion: 成功实现了无需修改NR标准且无需专用感知波形的ISAC系统，验证了高度可观测性解决方案的有效性，证明了ISAC技术在实际O-RAN架构中实现无人机检测与三维跟踪的可行性，为未来ISAC系统设计提供了有效参考。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) lets cellular infrastructure serve communication users and sense on the same waveform. We present an end-to-end O-RAN simulation testbed for 5G ISAC targeting low-altitude UAV detection and 3-D tracking, built from open-source components: OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC and Sionna RT, in which the NR Uplink Sounding Reference Signal is repurposed as a passive radar waveform: a PHY-layer sensing stage inside the gNB produces detections that reach an Extended Kalman Filter tracking xApp over a custom E2 service model, with no change to the NR standard and no dedicated sensing waveform. A single bistatic pair leaves elevation unobservable, so the tracker needs a height prior; we remove it two independent ways and measure both - a planar receive array supplying a vertical aperture, and a second transmitter supplying range diversity. Both live results corroborate an offline ray-traced study of the same estimator, which converges from a deliberately wrong initial altitude to 1.8 m RMSE at a consistent filter, so altitude observability is established both in the signal-processing chain and end to end through the live stack. Detection coverage is preserved under a concurrent 10 Mbps uplink communications load.

</details>


### [200] [BALANCE: Hybrid Autoregressive-Speculative LLM Inference in Wireless Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05926)
*Guanqiao Qu,Shuo Chen,Qian Chen,Kin K. Leung,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了BALANCE框架，通过混合自回归解码和推测解码，解决了边缘LLM推理中的延迟-内存权衡问题，显著提高了任务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘LLM推理中，自回归解码（AD）延迟长，推测解码（SD）虽能加速但增加内存开销。两种方法单独使用都无法在有限边缘计算资源下高效服务异构需求用户。

Method: 提出BALANCE框架，边缘服务器同时托管SLM和LLM，为每个用户分配AD或SD模式并同步执行。将任务吞吐量最大化建模为联合用户调度与计算资源分配问题，并开发多项式时间算法分解问题，获得常数近似保证的次优解。

Result: 实验表明，BALANCE在任务吞吐量上始终优于传统AD和SD方案。

Conclusion: BALANCE有效平衡延迟与内存成本，在受限边缘资源下显著提升了LLM推理服务的吞吐性能。

Abstract: Edge inference is a promising paradigm to provide large language model (LLM) inference services in next-generation mobile networks. LLM inference mainly relies on two approaches: Autoregressive decoding (AD) generates output tokens sequentially, resulting in long latency; Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates inference by using a small language model (SLM) to generate multiple draft tokens for LLM verification, but incurs extra memory costs. Due to this latency-memory tradeoff, neither approach alone can efficiently serve users with heterogeneous demands under limited edge computing resources. To address this challenge, we propose a hybrid autoregressive-speculative inference (BALANCE) framework for edge LLM inference. In BALANCE, an edge server hosts both an SLM and an LLM, assigns each user to AD or SD, and performs the two modes simultaneously. To maximize the number of served users, we formulate a task throughput maximization problem to jointly determine user scheduling and computing resource allocation between AD and SD under user latency requirements and server memory constraints. Since the problem is NP-hard, we develop a polynomial-time algorithm that transforms the original problem into two sub-problems and obtains a sub-optimal solution with a constant approximation guarantee. Experiments demonstrate that BALANCE consistently outperforms conventional AD and SD and significantly improves task throughput.

</details>


### [201] [ML-for-ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06046)
*Yutong Zhao,Noga H. Rotman,Gianni Antichi,Ran Ben Basat*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ML-for-ML跨层优化方法，通过联合优化网络侧和ML侧参数，在共享云集群中将达到目标损失的时间缩短了42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练工作负载快速增长，其时间、能源和基础设施成本日益重要。在共享云集群中，训练和微调作业与并发运行的工作负载竞争网络资源，而网络机制和ML训练选择通常单独优化：网络控制字节移动方式，ML系统控制通信发生的时间和数量。作者认为这种分离导致端到端性能未能充分利用。

Method: 提出ML-for-ML跨层视角，在共享的达到目标损失时间目标下联合选择网络侧和ML侧控制参数。构建了初步原型系统，实现ML和网络参数的协同优化。

Result: 通过协同优化ML和网络参数，达到目标损失的速度提升了42%。

Conclusion: 网络机制和ML训练选择的跨层联合优化能够显著提升AI训练的端到端性能，证明了打破网络层和ML层分离优化的必要性和有效性。

Abstract: AI training workloads are growing rapidly, making their time, energy, and infrastructure costs increasingly important. In shared cloud clusters, training and fine-tuning jobs compete with co-running workloads for network resources, while network mechanisms and ML training choices are typically optimized separately: networking controls how bytes move, whereas ML systems control when and how much communication occurs. We argue that this separation leaves end-to-end performance on the table.
  We present ML-for-ML, a cross-layer perspective in which network-side and ML-side knobs are selected jointly under a shared time-to-target-loss objective. Our preliminary prototype shows that by co-optimizing the ML and network parameters, we reach the target loss up to 42% faster.

</details>


### [202] [MARS: Multipath Adaptive Reliable Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06101)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARS，一种面向互联网/WAN环境的接收端驱动、转发器辅助的多路径传输方案，通过分层同步的覆盖路径发现和耦合的拥塞控制，在不改变IP路由的前提下实现了比纯端到端设计更好的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多路径传输方案存在实际权衡：端到端传输（如MPTCP、MPQUIC）易于部署但受限于端点可见路径和延迟的拥塞反馈；而路由或转发器辅助的方法通常需要基础设施支持或缺乏跨转发选择的安全协调。因此需要一种既易于部署又能充分利用路径多样性的多路径传输方案。

Method: MARS采用接收端驱动的转发器辅助架构，结合分层同步的覆盖路径发现机制与耦合的消费者/转发器拥塞控制。该方法能够安全地扩展可用的转发机会并在瓶颈附近快速响应。MARS作为增量可部署的UDP覆盖层运行于客户端、服务器、中继或CDN类节点上，无需改变底层IP路由。

Result: 在大规模仿真和Mininet仿真实验中，MARS表现出部署相关的优势：仅端点部署时与端到端多路径基线相当；配合协作覆盖转发器时，相比ECMP受限基线最大p95流完成时间降低达81.5%；即使与使用相同路径集的路径扩展端到端基线相比，MARS也实现了更低的worst-case p95 FCT、更强的丢包鲁棒性以及快速的瞬态链路故障恢复能力。

Conclusion: 研究证明ICN风格的接收端驱动转发可以作为可部署的覆盖传输层服务于WAN多路径，在不要求IP路由变更的情况下提供了超越纯端到端设计的性能收益，为大规模数据传输场景提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multipath transport is increasingly important for Internet/WAN services that move large data volumes across heterogeneous paths, including geo-distributed analytics, content distribution, and cloud-service pipelines. Existing solutions, however, face a practical trade-off: end-to-end transports such as MPTCP and MPQUIC are deployable but limited by endpoint-visible paths and delayed congestion feedback, while routing-or forwarder-assisted approaches often require infrastructure support or lack safe coordination across forwarding choices. This paper presents MARS, a receiver-driven, forwarder-assisted multipath transport for Internet/WAN environments. MARS combines tier-synchronized overlay path discovery with coupled consumer/forwarder congestion control, enabling it to safely expand usable forwarding opportunities and react near bottlenecks. It runs as an incrementally deployable UDP overlay at clients, servers, relays, or CDN-like nodes. We implement MARS in simulation and as a prototype, and evaluate it through large-scale simulation and Mininet emulation under different deployment scales, loss rates, and failure scenarios. The results show that MARS provides deployment-dependent benefits: with endpoint-only deployment, it remains competitive with end-to-end multipath baselines; with cooperating overlay forwarders, it exposes richer usable path diversity and reduces max p95 flow completion time by up to 81.5\% over ECMP-limited baselines. Even against path-expanded end-to-end baselines given the same path set, MARS achieves lower worst-case p95 FCT and stronger robustness under packet loss, while also recovering quickly from transient link failures. These results demonstrate that ICN-style receiver-driven forwarding can serve as a deployable overlay transport substrate for WAN multipath, providing benefits beyond purely end-to-end designs without requiring changes to IP routing.

</details>


### [203] [MultiMoQ: Multi-Access Media-Over-QUIC for Robust Immersive Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06102)
*Yitong Li,Xinjiao Li,Ruonan Chai,Dirk Kutscher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MultiMoQ，一个基于Media over QUIC (MoQ)的多接入分块流媒体框架，通过多路径传输和灵活的调度机制解决了大规模沉浸式视频流中高码率传输的鲁棒性问题，显著提升了传输性能和播放连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的沉浸式视频流方案在支持大规模并发用户的高码率传输时存在瓶颈，粗粒度的传输机制限制了响应性，且缺乏对并发分块流的协调支持。新兴的Media over QUIC (MoQ)方案虽然具备规模化媒体传输潜力，但在带宽受限条件下鲁棒性不足，易导致播放卡顿。这些局限性促使研究者寻求更优的解决方案。

Method: 提出MultiMoQ框架，在MoQ基础上重新设计传输机制：1) 支持多接入路径传输以提升带宽聚合能力和容错性；2) 设计灵活的分块(tile)调度策略；3) 实现无缝的接入切换机制。研究构建了完整原型系统，在异构真实网络条件的仿真环境中进行评估，与DASH和标准MoQ进行对比实验。

Result: MultiMoQ在传输性能上取得显著提升：增强了增强层分块和基础视频的吞吐量，相比DASH降低了增强层分块的尾部端到端延迟；保持了音频连续性并避免了标准MoQ中的持续卡顿问题。这些传输层改进直接转化为更平滑的视口播放体验，消融实验进一步验证了多接入控制对播放连续性的贡献。

Conclusion: MultiMoQ通过多接入路径聚合和优化的调度机制，成功解决了大规模沉浸式视频流中高码率传输的鲁棒性挑战，为虚拟活动、体育直播和远程教育等应用提供了可靠的技术支撑，证明了在MoQ架构中集成多路径传输的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Live immersive video streaming, particularly 360-degree video, is increasingly adopted in applications such as virtual events, sports broadcasting, and remote education. Existing approaches struggle to support high-bitrate immersive streaming for large numbers of concurrent users, with coarse-grained delivery limiting responsiveness and insufficient support for coordinating concurrent tile streams. Media over QUIC (MoQ) has recently emerged as a promising solution for large-scale media delivery, yet it lacks robustness under bandwidth-constrained conditions, often resulting in playback stalls. To address these challenges, we present MultiMoQ, a multi-access tile streaming framework built on MoQ that redesigns its delivery mechanism for robust high-bitrate streaming across multiple access paths while supporting flexible tile scheduling and seamless access switching. We implement a fully functional prototype of MultiMoQ and evaluate it in network emulation under heterogeneous real-world network conditions, comparing against Dynamic Adaptive Streaming over HTTP (DASH) and standard MoQ. Results show that MultiMoQ increases goodput for enhancement tiles and base video and reduces enhancement-tile tail end-to-end latency relative to DASH, while preserving audio continuity and avoiding the persistent stalls of standard MoQ. These transport gains translate into smoother viewport playback, and the ablation results further confirm the contribution of multi-access control to playback continuity.

</details>


### [204] [From Passive Mirrors to Active Agents: Holonic Digital Twins for Physical AI over Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06227)
*Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Omar Hashash,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了Holonic数字孪生网络(HDT-Net)框架，通过分层主动推理和因果协调解决AI在物理系统中的失效问题，实现实时物理智能协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI工具（包括深度学习和生成式AI）在嵌入物理系统（如机器人和车辆）时仍然失败，因为无法在不确定性下维护可靠的世界模型进行长期规划，且难以泛化到未见过的场景。现有无线网络架构优化吞吐量、延迟和可靠性，但无法支持需要维护共享时空上下文的实时物理AI协调。

Method: 提出Holonic数字孪生网络(HDT-Net)框架，每个HDT为跨越物理代理和网络边缘的层次结构，在本地自主推理并与邻近HDT协作。使用因果马尔可夫毯确定需要协调的代理并实现跨域干预的反事实推理。采用主动推理统一感知、动作和学习，基于认知价值决定传输内容。范畴理论确保跨异构代理的语义结构保持，整合信息理论量化集体智能涌现。

Result: HDT-Net实现了实时物理AI推理，通过主动推理最小化期望自由能来统一感知、动作和学习，范畴理论保证了异构代理间的语义结构保持，整合信息理论成功量化了集体智能何时超越独立运行以及网络智能如何通过协调学习和信息交换演化。

Conclusion: HDT-Net框架成功解决了AI在物理系统中的关键挑战，通过holonic代理的主动推理而非被动镜像物理资产，实现了真正的物理智能编排，为无线网络支持实时物理AI协调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Despite advances in artificial intelligence (AI) across multiple sectors, today's AI tools, including deep learning and generative AI, still fail when embedded into physical systems, such as robots and vehicles operating under real-world physical laws. This stems from their inability to maintain reliable world models for long-horizon planning under uncertainty and generalize to unseen scenarios. In this context, wireless networks, through pervasive sensing and communication, can orchestrate physical intelligence. However, current architectures optimize throughput, latency, and reliability and cannot support real-time physical AI coordination, requiring agents to maintain shared spatiotemporal context. To address these challenges, a network of holonic digital twins (HDT-Nets) framework is proposed to deliver real-time physical AI inference through holonic agents that actively reason about their environment rather than passively mirror physical assets. Each HDT is realized as a hierarchical structure spanning the physical agent and network edge, reasoning autonomously at the local level while cooperating with neighboring HDTs to form collectively intelligent units. In HDT-Net, causal Markov blankets spanning sensing, communication, and control determine which agents must coordinate and enable counterfactual reasoning over multi-domain interventions. Active inference within these boundaries unifies perception, action, and learning by minimizing expected free energy while deciding which beliefs to transmit based on their cognitive value to the receiver. Category theory ensures that transmitted beliefs preserve semantic structure across heterogeneous agents with incompatible representations. Finally, integrated information theory quantifies when collective intelligence exceeds independent operation and how network intelligence evolves through coordinated learning and information exchange.

</details>
